概念图生成\")\n43\t# 等待 Director 审批后\n44\tpoll_secret_request(request_id=\"<返回的 request_id>\", project=\"&lt;project_name>\")\n45\t```\n46\t\n47\t如果密钥尚未存储，告知 Director 需要 GOOGLE_API_KEY。\n48\t\n49\t### Step --version\n57\t}\n58\t```\n59\t\n60\t如果 `paperbanana --version` 失败（命令不存在），必须先 `pip install paperbanana -q` 安装。版本需 ≥ 0.1.2。\n61\t\n62\t**设计前必读** `skills/global/paper-visualization.skill.md` 的概念图 L1（防丑 已废弃，不指定会崩溃）。**`--iterations 5`**。\n67\t\n68\t## 完整流程\n69\t\n70\t### Step 1：写意图描述文件\n71\t\n72\t将详细设计描述写入文件（太长不适合命令行参数）：\n73\t\n74\t```bash\n75\tcat > /tmp/fig_intent.txt << 'INTENT'\n76\tThree-panel academic figure for a top-venue paper (full width, aspect ratio ~3.2:1, figsize 14x4.5 inches):\n77\t\n78\tPanel (a) 'The Phenomenon' (left, ~38% width):\n79\tGrouped bar chart. TF bars: dark navy (#2C3E50). AR bars: academic red (#C0392B).\n80\tExact data: Base TF=0.501 AR=0.019, ThreadGate TF=0.504 AR=0.064, ...\n81\tY-axis 'Mid-turn GAR' range 0–0.55. Only left+bottom spines. Serif font 10pt.\n82\tAdd bracket annotation '8-17x gap' between bar groups.\n83\t\n84\tPanel (b) 'Diagnostic Chain' (center, ~24% width):\n85\tVertical flowchart, 4 rounded boxes connected by thin gray arrows:\n86\t  Step 1: light gray (#F5F6F7) box, thin border (#DEE2E6), 'Gate Analysis' + subtitle. Green ✓\n87\t  Step 2: same style, 'PCGrad Exclusion'. Red ✗\n88\t  Step 3: same style, 'Representation Check'. Red ✗\n89\t  Step 4: light green (#D4EDDA) background, 'Conclusion: Exposure bias'. Green ✓\n90\tNo axes, no spines. Clean infographic style.\n91\t\n92\tPanel (c) 'Constructive Evidence' (right, ~38% width):\n93\tLine plot. Data points: (0, 0.064), (3, 0.556), (5, 0.727).\n94\tNavy line (#2C3E50), open circles (white fill, navy edge, width 2).\n95\tLight blue fill (#EBF5FB, alpha 0.4) under curve.\n96\tAnnotate '11x recovery' in muted gray, 9pt italic.\n97\t\n98\tOVERALL STYLE:\n99\t- 300 DPI, white background, publication quality\n100\t- Serif font (Times New Roman or similar) throughout\n101\t- Panel labels '(a)', '(b)', '(c)' in 11pt bold at top-left\n102\t- Generous white space (wspace=0.35)\n103\t- Muted academic palette: navy #2C3E50, red #C0392B, green #27AE60, gray #7F8C8D\n104\t- NO matplotlib default styling: no gray background, no thick gridlines, no tab10 colors\n105\t- Drop shadows on boxes: subtle, 2px offset, 10% opacity\n106\t- Rounded corners 8px on all rectangles\n107\t- Arrow heads: small, elegant, not bulky\n108\tINTENT\n109\t```\n110\t\n111\t### Step 2：运行 generate\n112\t\n113\t```bash\n114\tGOOGLE_API_KEY=\"$GOOGLE_API_KEY\" paperbanana generate \\\n115\t  --input /tmp/fig_intent.txt \\\n116\t  --caption \"Three-panel diagnostic framework: (a) TF vs AR performance gap, (b) systematic diagnosis chain, (c) beta-steering recovery evidence\" \\\n117\t  --vlm-model gemini-2.5-pro \\\n118\t  --output figures/paper/fig1_overview.png \\\n119\t  --iterations 5\n120\t```\n121\t\n122\t### Step 3：视觉检查（不可跳过）\n123\t\n124\t**Read 生成的 PNG 文件**，视觉评估以下维度：\n125\t\n126\t1. **高级感**：是否有顶会论文的精致感？还是看起来像课程作业？\n127\t2. **颜色**：是否有语义（不是随机分配）？是否和谐、不刺眼？\n128\t3. **文字**：缩到 column width 后是否 ≥ 7pt 可读？\n129\t4. **布局**：留白是否合理？元素是否对齐？\n130\t5. **细节**：圆角、阴影、箭头是否精致？边框是否统一？\n131\t\n132\t**不满意 → 必须修改意图描述文件，重新生成。**\n133\t\n134\t### Step 4：转 PDF\n135\t\n136\t```bash\n137\tpython3 -c \"\n138\timport matplotlib; matplotlib.use('Agg')\n139\timport matplotlib.pyplot as plt, matplotlib.image as mpimg\n140\timg = mpimg.imread('figures/paper/fig1_overview.png')\n141\th, w = img.shape[:2]\n142\tfig = plt.figure(figsize=(w/300, h/300), dpi=300)\n143\tax = fig.add_axes([0,0,1,1]); ax.imshow(img); ax.axis('off')\n144\tplt.savefig('figures/paper/fig1_overview.pdf', bbox_inches='tight', pad_inches=0)\n145\tplt.close()\n146\t\"\n147\t```\n148\t\n149\t## 意图描述质量红线\n150\t\n151\tIntent 质量直接决定图的质量。必须包含以下 7 项（缺一不可）：\n152\t\n153\t1. **空间布局**：每个 panel 的宽度占比（如 38%/24%/38%）\n154\t2. **组件形状 + 尺寸**：圆角半径 8px、figsize (14, 4.5)\n155\t3. **颜色 hex 码**：不说\"蓝色\"，说 \"#2C3E50\"，绑定语义\n156\t4. **所有文字内容**：box label、副标题、箭头 annotation\n157\t5. **尺寸规格**：DPI 300、字号 10-12pt body / 8-9pt labels\n158\t6. **细节规格**：阴影偏移 2px / 10% opacity、箭头粗细、边框宽度\n159\t7. **反面约束**：\"NO matplotlib default styling, NO gray background, NO tab10 colors\"\n160\t\n161\t### 美学风格关键词（必须在 intent 中出现）\n162\t\n163\t```\n164\tSTYLE REQUIREMENTS:\n165\t- Publication quality for a TOP venue (NeurIPS/ICML/EMNLP)\n166\t- Elegant, premium, sophisticated — NOT cheap or homework-like\n167\t- Subtle drop shadows (2px, 10% opacity) on boxes\n168\t- Rounded corners (8px) on all rectangles\n169\t- Thin, elegant arrow heads — not bulky matplotlib defaults\n170\t- Serif font (Times) for labels, sans-serif (Helvetica) for annotations\n171\t- Muted, harmonious color palette — NO saturated primary colors\n172\t- Generous white space between elements\n173\t- Consistent visual weight across all panels\n174\t```\n175\t\n176\t### 反面教材（这样的 intent 一定画不好）\n177\t\n178\t- \"Draw a pipeline diagram with three stages\"\n179\t- \"Architecture diagram for our system\"\n180\t\n181\t太模糊。LLM 会用 matplotlib 默认样式填充所有你没指定的细节，结果就是廉价感。\n182\t\n183\t## 迭代策略（至少 3 轮外部迭代，Hero Figure 至少 5 轮）\n184\t\n185\tPaperBanana 内部有 VLM Critic 迭代（`--iterations 5`），但这还不够。你必须做**外部迭代**：\n186\t\n187\t### 第 1 轮：基础生成\n188\t运行 PaperBanana → Read PNG → 识别大方向问题（布局、配色、结构）\n189\t\n190\t### 第 2 轮：结构修复\n191\t修改意图描述（布局占比、颜色 hex、组件关系）→ 重新运行 → Read PNG\n192\t\n193\t### 第 3 轮：细节打磨\n194\t修改意图描述（字号、阴影、对齐、箭头粗细）→ 重新运行 → Read PNG\n195\t\n196\t### 第 4 轮（Hero Figure）：风格统一\n197\t线宽 / 字号 / 间距全图统一？与论文其他图配色一致？→ 重新运行 → Read PNG\n198\t\n199\t### 第 5 轮（Hero Figure）：最终 polish\n200\t像素级检查 → 缩到 column width 后文字 ≥ 7pt？→ 终极判断：放在 NeurIPS best paper 里违和吗？\n201\t\n202\t### 判断标准\n203\t\n204\t**可以交付**：看起来像 NeurIPS 2025 best paper 里的图。\n205\t**不可交付**：看起来像 matplotlib gallery 的示例图。\n206\t\n207\t## 失败回退\n208\t\n209\t如果 PaperBanana 多次崩溃（API 错误、模型不兼容）：\n210\t\n211\t1. 确认 `--vlm-model gemini-2.5-pro` 是否指定\n212\t2. 确认 GOOGLE_API_KEY 是否有效：`curl -s -o /dev/null -w '%{http_code}' \"https://generativelanguage.googleapis.com/v1beta/models?key=$GOOGLE_API_KEY\"` 应返回 200\n213\t3. 如果仍然失败，回退 matplotlib 手写：\n214\t   - Read `skills/global/paper-visualization.skill.md` 获取 L1-L4 设计标准\n215\t   - 写 Python 脚本手动画概念图（FancyBboxPatch、FancyArrowPatch 等）\n216\t   - **同样必须 Read PNG 做视觉检查，至少迭代 3 轮**\n217\t   - **同样必须达到顶会级别的美观度**\n218\t   - 保存 .py 脚本到 figures/paper/ --version\n}\n```\n\n如果 `paperbanana --version` 失败（命令不存在），必须先 `pip install paperbanana -q` 安装。版本需 ≥ 0.1.2。\n\n**设计前必读** `skills/global/paper-visualization.skill.md` 的概念图 L1（防丑 7 条）+ L2（叙事 6 条）+ L3 Pattern + Checklist。统计图部分不适用于 PaperBanana。\n\n## 命令：generate\n\n概念图用 `generate` 子命令。**必须显式指定 `--vlm-model gemini-2.5-pro`**（默认模型 `gemini-2.0-flash` 已废弃，不指定会崩溃）。**`--iterations 5`**。\n\n## 完整流程\n\n### Step 1：写意图描述文件\n\n将详细设计描述写入文件（太长不适合命令行参数）：\n\n```bash\ncat > /tmp/fig_intent.txt << 'INTENT'\nThree-panel academic figure for a top-venue paper (full width, aspect ratio ~3.2:1, figsize 14x4.5 inches):\n\nPanel (a) 'The Phenomenon' (left, ~38% width):\nGrouped bar chart. TF bars: dark navy (#2C3E50). AR bars: academic red (#C0392B).\nExact data: Base TF=0.501 AR=0.019, ThreadGate TF=0.504 AR=0.064, ...\nY-axis 'Mid-turn GAR' range 0–0.55. Only left+bottom spines. Serif font 10pt.\nAdd bracket annotation '8-17x gap' between bar groups.\n\nPanel (b) 'Diagnostic Chain' (center, ~24% width):\nVertical flowchart, 4 rounded boxes connected by thin gray arrows:\n  Step 1: light gray (#F5F6F7) box, thin border (#DEE2E6), 'Gate Analysis' + subtitle. Green ✓\n  Step 2: same style, 'PCGrad Exclusion'. Red ✗\n  Step 3: same style, 'Representation Check'. Red ✗\n  Step 4: light green (#D4EDDA) background, 'Conclusion: Exposure bias'. Green ✓\nNo axes, no spines. Clean infographic style.\n\nPanel (c) 'Constructive Evidence' (right, ~38% width):\nLine plot. Data points: (0, 0.064), (3, 0.556), (5, 0.727).\nNavy line (#2C3E50), open circles (white fill, navy edge, width 2).\nLight blue fill (#EBF5FB, alpha 0.4) under curve.\nAnnotate '11x recovery' in muted gray, 9pt italic.\n\nOVERALL STYLE:\n- 300 DPI, white background, publication quality\n- Serif font (Times New Roman or similar) throughout\n- Panel labels '(a)', '(b)', '(c)' in 11pt bold at top-left\n- Generous white space (wspace=0.35)\n- Muted academic palette: navy #2C3E50, red #C0392B, green #27AE60, gray #7F8C8D\n- NO matplotlib default styling: no gray background, no thick gridlines, no tab10 colors\n- Drop shadows on boxes: subtle, 2px offset, 10% opacity\n- Rounded corners 8px on all rectangles\n- Arrow heads: small, elegant, not bulky\nINTENT\n```\n\n### Step 2：运行 generate\n\n```bash\nGOOGLE_API_KEY=\"$GOOGLE_API_KEY\" paperbanana generate \\\n  --input /tmp/fig_intent.txt \\\n  --caption \"Three-panel diagnostic framework: (a) TF vs AR performance gap, (b) systematic diagnosis chain, (c) beta-steering recovery evidence\" \\\n  --vlm-model gemini-2.5-pro \\\n  --output figures/paper/fig1_overview.png \\\n  --iterations 5\n```\n\n### Step 3：视觉检查（不可跳过）\n\n**Read 生成的 PNG 文件**，视觉评估以下维度：\n\n1. **高级感**：是否有顶会论文的精致感？还是看起来像课程作业？\n2. **颜色**：是否有语义（不是随机分配）？是否和谐、不刺眼？\n3. **文字**：缩到 column width 后是否 ≥ 7pt 可读？\n4. **布局**：留白是否合理？元素是否对齐？\n5. **细节**：圆角、阴影、箭头是否精致？边框是否统一？\n\n**不满意 → 必须修改意图描述文件，重新生成。**\n\n### Step 4：转 PDF\n\n```bash\npython3 -c \"\nimport matplotlib; matplotlib.use('Agg')\nimport matplotlib.pyplot as plt, matplotlib.image as mpimg\nimg = mpimg.imread('figures/paper/fig1_overview.png')\nh, w = img.shape[:2]\nfig = plt.figure(figsize=(w/300, h/300), dpi=300)\nax = fig.add_axes([0,0,1,1]); ax.imshow(img); ax.axis('off')\nplt.savefig('figures/paper/fig1_overview.pdf', bbox_inches='tight', pad_inches=0)\nplt.close()\n\"\n```\n\n## 意图描述质量红线\n\nIntent 质量直接决定图的质量。必须包含以下 7 项（缺一不可）：\n\n1. **空间布局**：每个 panel 的宽度占比（如 38%/24%/38%）\n2. **组件形状 + 尺寸**：圆角半径 8px、figsize (14, 4.5)\n3. **颜色 hex 码**：不说\"蓝色\"，说 \"#2C3E50\"，绑定语义\n4. **所有文字内容**：box label、副标题、箭头 annotation\n5. **尺寸规格**：DPI 300、字号 10-12pt body / 8-9pt labels\n6. **细节规格**：阴影偏移 2px / 10% opacity、箭头粗细、边框宽度\n7. **反面约束**：\"NO matplotlib default styling, NO gray background, NO tab10 colors\"\n\n### 美学风格关键词（必须在 intent 中出现）\n\n```\nSTYLE REQUIREMENTS:\n- Publication quality for a TOP venue (NeurIPS/ICML/EMNLP)\n- Elegant, premium, sophisticated — NOT cheap or homework-like\n- Subtle drop shadows (2px, 10% opacity) on boxes\n- Rounded corners (8px) on all rectangles\n- Thin, elegant arrow heads — not bulky matplotlib defaults\n- Serif font (Times) for labels, sans-serif (Helvetica) for annotations\n- Muted, harmonious color palette — NO saturated primary colors\n- Generous white space between elements\n- Consistent visual weight across all panels\n```\n\n### 反面教材（这样的 intent 一定画不好）\n\n- \"Draw a pipeline diagram with three stages\"\n- \"Architecture diagram for our system\"\n\n太模糊。LLM 会用 matplotlib 默认样式填充所有你没指定的细节，结果就是廉价感。\n\n## 迭代策略（至少 3 轮外部迭代，Hero Figure 至少 5 轮）\n\nPaperBanana 内部有 VLM Critic 迭代（`--iterations 5`），但这还不够。你必须做**外部迭代**：\n\n### 第 1 轮：基础生成\n运行 PaperBanana → Read PNG → 识别大方向问题（布局、配色、结构）\n\n### 第 2 轮：结构修复\n修改意图描述（布局占比、颜色 hex、组件关系）→ 重新运行 → Read PNG\n\n### 第 3 轮：细节打磨\n修改意图描述（字号、阴影、对齐、箭头粗细）→ 重新运行 → Read PNG\n\n### 第 4 轮（Hero Figure）：风格统一\n线宽 / 字号 / 间距全图统一？与论文其他图配色一致？→ 重新运行 → Read PNG\n\n### 第 5 轮（Hero Figure）：最终 polish\n像素级检查 → 缩到 column width 后文字 ≥ 7pt？→ 终极判断：放在 NeurIPS best paper 里违和吗？\n\n### 判断标准\n\n**可以交付**：看起来像 NeurIPS 2025 best paper 里的图。\n**不可交付**：看起来像 matplotlib gallery 的示例图。\n\n## 失败回退\n\n如果 PaperBanana 多次崩溃（API 错误、模型不兼容）：\n\n1. 确认 `--vlm-model gemini-2.5-pro` 是否指定\n2. 确认 GOOGLE_API_KEY 是否有效：`curl -s -o /dev/null -w '%{http_code}' \"https://generativelanguage.googleapis.com/v1beta/models?key=$GOOGLE_API_KEY\"` 应返回 200\n3. 如果仍然失败，回退 matplotlib 手写：\n   - Read `skills/global/paper-visualization.skill.md` 获取 L1-L4 设计标准\n   - 写 Python 脚本手动画概念图（FancyBboxPatch、FancyArrowPatch 等）\n   - **同样必须 Read PNG 做视觉检查，至少迭代 3 轮**\n   - **同样必须达到顶会级别的美观度**\n   - 保存 .py 脚本到 figures/paper/ 目录便于后续修改\n","numLines":219,"startLine":1,"totalLines":219}}}
{"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01TLqLZzWEufyMpiBMHogmxK","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t---\n2\tdescription: 学术论文图表设计与生成——四层指南（防丑原则 + 叙事原则 + Pattern 库 + 工具路由）。Paper Agent 规划 visualization_plan 时读，Worker 执行时也读。\n3\tname: paper-visualization\n4\tscope: global\n5\ttags:\n6\t- research\n7\t- visualization\n8\t- paper\n9\t- matplotlib\n10\t- paperbanana\n11\tversion: 5\n12\troles:\n13\t- agent\n14\t- paper_agent\n15\t---\n16\t\n17\t# Paper Visualization — 学术图表设计与生成\n18\t\n19\t> **核心理念**：好图不是\"画得对\"，是\"回答一个问题\"。每张图动笔前先问：**这张图要让读者看到什么？**\n20\t>\n21\t> **质量底线**：每张图必须达到 NeurIPS/ICML/EMNLP best paper 的视觉水准——高级感、专业感、精致感。绝不接受 matplotlib 默认样式的廉价感。\n22\t\n23\t本 skill 分五层：\n24\t\n25\t- **L0 学习同行** — 设计前看几张优秀同类型论文图，比读任何原则都有效\n26\t- **L1 防丑原则** — 底线，所有图必须遵守\n27\t- **L2 叙事原则** — 让图有说服力（区分\"数据图\"和\"论证图\"）\n28\t- **L3 Pattern 库** — 具体技法目录，按 `design_notes` 选用\n29\t- **L4 工具路由 + 模板** — paperbanana vs matplotlib，常用统计图模板\n30\t\n31\t---\n32\t\n33\t## L0 学习同行（设计前的功课）\n34\t\n35\t你的子领域里，**最被引用、最被记住的论文是怎么画图的？** 在动笔之前花点时间去看一眼——这个动作的回报远高于读完下面的 L1-L4。\n36\t\n37\t### 为什么这一步是重要的\n38\t\n39\t- 同领域读者已经被这些论文的视觉语言\"训练\"过——遵循惯例 = 降低读者认知负担\n40\t- 顶会论文经历过 reviewer 严苛审查，能登出来的图基本说明它的 narrative 设计是被接受的\n41\t- 抽象原则告诉你\"用 fill_between 表示发散\"，但好论文告诉你\"在这种数据上，发散到底应该画多淡才合适\"\n42\t- 一个领域往往有它特有的图表惯例（如校准研究的 reliability diagram、scaling 研究的 log-log curve、RL 的 learning curve with seed bands），不看就不会用\n43\t\n44\t### 什么时候值得投入\n45\t\n46\t判断权交给你。一些可参考的信号：\n47\t\n48\t- **值得多花时间**：会决定论文成败的图（hero figure、main result、新颖的实验类型）\n49\t- **不太值得**：常规 supplementary 图、loss curve 这类标准化已经很高的图\n50\t- **强烈建议看**：你打算画一种自己不熟悉的图，或者数据形态是这个领域少见的\n51\t\n52\t### 怎么看\n53\t\n54\t工具：`paper-image-extractor` skill 可以从 arXiv source 或 PDF 提取参考论文里的 figure。你已经有 `literature_map.yaml` 里的论文清单作为起点。\n55\t\n56\t看的时候问自己（不是机械检查清单，是真的去理解）：\n57\t\n58\t- 这张图回答了什么问题？读者一眼能 get 到吗？\n59\t- 它用了哪些叙事层（background band / 参考线 / 概念曲线 / fill 区域）？这些层在我的数据上有对应物吗？\n60\t- 它的颜色和 marker 怎么承载语义？我的图能不能借鉴这套映射？\n61\t- 它有什么是这篇论文特有的（与他们的具体数据 / claim 绑死）？这些不要照搬。\n62\t- 如果让我重画它的图，我会怎么改？为什么作者没这么做？\n63\t\n64\t### 怎么把学到的传下去\n65\t\n66\t把你看完后的判断**直接写进对应 fig 的 `design_notes`**，用人话不用模板。例子：\n67\t\n68\t```yaml\n69\tdesign_notes:\n70\t  - \"学 GPTQ Fig 4：用 log-scale bit-width + dashed FP16 baseline + 不同 marker shape 分模型族\"\n71\t  - \"Pattern A（regime band）划分 in-domain / OOD 两段\"\n72\t  - \"AWQ Fig 2 那种 hatching 区分 outlier 不适用我们的数据，跳过\"\n73\t```\n74\t\n75\tWorker 拿到这种 design_notes 就知道既要参考 GPTQ 的 layout 又要用 Pattern A，能直接落实成代码。\n76\t\n77\t### 边界\n78\t\n79\t- 不要复刻，要消化。直接抄一张图既没用（数据不一样）也有风险（与他们的 claim 强绑定）\n80\t- 不要看太多。3 张就够，多了 Paper Agent 会陷入\"该听谁的\"\n81\t- 看不到原图（提取失败 / arXiv 无 source）就跳过，不要在这里卡住流程\n82\t\n83\t---\n84\t\n85\t## L1 防丑原则（底线，不可违反）\n86\t\n87\t来自 Tufte 和 Luís Cruz 16 条公认规范。Agent 默认遵守，无需逐条思考。\n88\t\n89\t1. **Self-explanatory** — 不看正文就能看懂：title/caption + 所有轴有 label + 单位\n90\t2. **去 chartjunk** — 不要 3D、不要背景框、不要无意义网格、不要 dual y-axis、不要 pie/donut chart\n91\t3. **直接标注 > Legend** — 能直接标在曲线/柱旁就不用 legend；legend 必要时不遮数据\n92\t4. **颜色/形状/纹理只用于 data variation** — 不用颜色装饰，不用相同颜色表示不同数据\n93\t5. **Meaningful baseline** — bar chart 必须从 0 起；scatter 可以非 0\n94\t6. **文字水平** — label 不要旋转/垂直；类别名太长 → 用 horizontal bar chart\n95\t7. **Sort for comparison** — 柱状图按数值排序，便于对比（除非有自然顺序如时间）\n96\t8. **字号一致** — 用 mm 计算 figsize（`ONE_MM = 1/25.4`），不在 LaTeX 里 `\\includegraphics[scale=...]` resize\n97\t9. **色盲友好** — 默认 Okabe-Ito；红/绿区分时变 saturation；考虑 grayscale 打印\n98\t10. **Bold 只用于强调** — 不要全图加粗；highlight 留给 takeaway\n99\t11. **Line plot 只用于有序数据** — 类别变量用 bar\n100\t12. **单 x 单 y 轴** — 必须双轴时改成 multi-panel\n101\t13. **300 DPI、PDF 矢量、savefig.bbox=tight、去掉 top/right spines**\n102\t\n103\t---\n104\t\n105\t## L2 叙事原则（让图变成论证）\n106\t\n107\tL1 让图\"不丑\"，L2 让图\"有说服力\"。这一层是 Agent 自主画图时最容易缺失的。\n108\t\n109\t### 原则 1：每张图回答一个明确问题\n110\t\n111\t动笔前用一句话写下：\"This figure shows that ___.\"\n112\t\n113\t如果写不出来，就不要画。如果写出来的 takeaway 不是论文的 claim，就要重新设计。\n114\t\n115\t**反例**：`\"Accuracy across methods\"` — 这只是个标题，不是问题\n116\t**正例**：`\"FP16 baseline 在小模型上比量化版高 5pp，但 70B 模型上差距消失\"` — 这是一张图要说服读者相信的事\n117\t\n118\t`visualization_plan.{fig_id}.narrative_intent` 字段写这一句。\n119\t\n120\t### 原则 2：颜色绑定语义而非数据顺序\n121\t\n122\t错的做法：`colors = ['blue', 'orange', 'green', 'red']` 按数据顺序分配。\n123\t对的做法：`BN → 蓝色（始终）`、`non-BN → 橙色（始终）`、`collapse/danger → vermillion (#D55E00)`、`safe/baseline → blue (#0072B2)`。\n124\t\n125\t整篇论文 6 张图，BN 永远蓝、collapse 永远红——零认知负担，读者一眼建立映射。\n126\t\n127\t### 原则 3：信息层级用 visual variable 表达\n128\t\n129\t数据的\"质量\"和\"分类\"应该可见，不应该藏在 caption 里：\n130\t\n131\t| 信息 | Visual 表达 |\n132\t|------|------------|\n133\t| n < 50 低置信度点 | hollow marker + 标注 `n=` |\n134\t| Out-of-sample / pending 数据 | hollow + 不同 marker shape（如 diamond）+ \"OOS\" label |\n135\t| Collapse / 异常类别 | hatching（斜线填充）|\n136\t| 主结果 vs 次要结果 | 主用饱和色，次要用 muted 色或 alpha |\n137\t\n138\t### 原则 4：加叙事层，不只画数据\n139\t\n140\t数据是图的基础，叙事层是让读者\"读懂\"的关键。常见叙事层：\n141\t\n142\t- **Background bands** — `axhspan/axvspan` 标定性区域（\"collapse zone\"、\"safe zone\"、\"Regime A/B/C\"）\n143\t- **Fill_between** — 显示两条曲线发散区域（alpha 极低如 0.06，不抢眼但有面积）\n144\t- **Reference lines** — 锚定真实值（如 dashed line 标\"actual W3A16 accuracy\"）\n145\t- **Equivalence band** — `axvspan` gradient fade 标 +/-1pp 等价区间\n146\t- **Concept curve** — 完全合成的曲线（如 cosine trajectory）建立理论直觉\n147\t\n148\t这些都不来自数据，但帮读者\"看到\"数据想说什么。\n149\t\n150\t### 原则 5：Subtitle / panel label 写 insight 不写描述\n151\t\n152\t错：`\"Layer 6 comparison across three models\"`\n153\t对：`\"Same relative position, opposite function\"`\n154\t\n155\t错：`\"Reliability diagram\"`\n156\t对：`\"Quantization shifts confidence but not accuracy\"`\n157\t\n158\t副标题是免费的 takeaway，不要浪费。\n159\t\n160\t### 原则 6：Multi-panel 每个面板独立回答一个 sub-question\n161\t\n162\t不要做\"主图 + 装饰小图\"。每个 panel 必须有独立的信息价值。\n163\t\n164\t如果一个 panel 想不出独立的问题，要么删掉，要么合并到主 panel。\n165\t\n166\t布局上：非对称比例（如 1.4:1）让视觉重心对齐叙事重心。\n167\t\n168\t### 原则 7：所有图至少 3 轮迭代，Hero figure 至少 5 轮\n169\t\n170\t第一版画出来基本不能直接用。计划好预算：先确定数据和布局，再调样式标注，最后 polish。不要指望一次到位。\n171\t\n172\t### Hero Figure 定义\n173\t\n174\tHero Figure = Figure 1 + 主结果图（main result figure）。这两类图对论文第一印象影响最大，必须投入更多迭代。\n175\t\n176\t---\n177\t\n178\t## L3 Pattern 库（按 `design_notes` 选用）\n179\t\n180\t按\"图要回答的问题\"选 pattern。每个 pattern 给出**何时用**、**怎么用**（具体代码或 matplotlib API）。\n181\t\n182\t### Pattern A：定性区域 / regime bands\n183\t\n184\t**何时用**：x 或 y 轴有自然分段（regime、phase、severity tier）。\n185\t\n186\t```python\n187\t# horizontal bands\n188\tfor lo, hi, color, label in [(0, 1, '#EBF5FB', 'Regime A'), (1, 5, '#EAFAF1', 'Regime B')]:\n189\t    ax.axhspan(lo, hi, alpha=0.25, color=color, zorder=0)\n190\t    ax.text(x_label_pos, (lo+hi)/2, label, va='center', fontsize=9, color='gray')\n191\t\n192\t# 单一 collapse zone\n193\tax.axhspan(-5, 5, color='#FDEDEC', alpha=0.5, zorder=0)\n194\tax.text(0.02, 2.5, 'collapse zone', fontsize=8, color='#ccaaaa', fontstyle='italic')\n195\t```\n196\t\n197\t### Pattern B：发散面积 fill_between\n198\t\n199\t**何时用**：两组数据从某点开始发散，想让\"发散程度\"可见。\n200\t\n201\t```python\n202\t# alpha 0.06 极淡，不抢眼但有面积\n203\tax.fill_between(x_range, curve_high, curve_low, alpha=0.06, color='#555555', zorder=1)\n204\t```\n205\t\n206\t### Pattern C：参考线锚定真实值\n207\t\n208\t**何时用**：合成 / 缩放后的 x 轴需要锚定到真实物理量。\n209\t\n210\t```python\n211\tax.axhline(y=actual_value, color=color, linestyle=':', linewidth=0.8, alpha=0.3)\n212\tt = ax.text(x_pos, actual_value+1, 'actual W3A16', fontsize=7, color=color, alpha=0.5)\n213\tt.set_path_effects([pe.withStroke(linewidth=2.5, foreground='white')])  # 防遮挡\n214\t```\n215\t\n216\t### Pattern D：信息层级 marker（hollow vs solid）\n217\t\n218\t**何时用**：同一图中有不同置信度 / OOS / pending 数据。\n219\t\n220\t```python\n221\t# 实心 = n >= 50, 空心 = n < 50\n222\tif n < 50:\n223\t    ax.plot(x, y, marker='o', markerfacecolor='white', markeredgecolor=color,\n224\t            markeredgewidth=1.2, markersize=6)\n225\t    ax.annotate(f'n={n}', (x, y), xytext=(0, 7), textcoords='offset points',\n226\t                fontsize=5, color=color, fontstyle='italic')\n227\t```\n228\t\n229\t### Pattern E：Wilson CI / bootstrap 不确定性\n230\t\n231\t**何时用**：accuracy / proportion 数据需要置信区间。\n232\t\n233\t```python\n234\tdef wilson_ci(p, n, z=1.96):\n235\t    denom = 1 + z**2 / n\n236\t    center = (p + z**2 / (2*n)) / denom\n237\t    half = z * np.sqrt((p*(1-p) + z**2/(4*n)) / n) / denom\n238\t    return max(0, center - half), min(1, center + half)\n239\t```\n240\t\n241\t### Pattern F：Per-label offset dict（解决标注重叠）\n242\t\n243\t**何时用**：scatter 上多个点需要标注且自动布局重叠。\n244\t\n245\t```python\n246\tlabel_offsets = {\n247\t    'Qwen3-8B':  (6, -12),   # 向下避让\n248\t    'Qwen3-4B':  (-55, 4),   # 向左避让\n249\t    'Llama-8B':  (6, 6),\n250\t}\n251\tfor name, (x, y) in data.items():\n252\t    ox, oy = label_offsets.get(name, (6, 6))\n253\t    ax.annotate(name, (x, y), xytext=(ox, oy), textcoords='offset points', fontsize=7)\n254\t```\n255\t\n256\t### Pattern G：Hatching 加分类维度\n257\t\n258\t**何时用**：已用颜色表示一个维度，再加一个二元维度（如 collapse vs not）。\n259\t\n260\t```python\n261\tfor is_collapse, bar in zip(collapse_flags, bars):\n262\t    if is_collapse:\n263\t        bar.set_hatch('///')\n264\t        bar.set_alpha(0.7)\n265\t```\n266\t\n267\t### Pattern H：Multi-panel 非对称布局\n268\t\n269\t**何时用**：主图 + 辅助 panel；或两个等重要 panel 但视觉重心需要倾斜。\n270\t\n271\t```python\n272\tfig, axes = plt.subplots(1, 2, figsize=(6.5, 3.2),\n273\t                          gridspec_kw={'width_ratios': [1.4, 1]})\n274\t# 主面板 1.4x 宽，副面板 1x；视觉重心在左\n275\t```\n276\t\n277\t### Pattern I：Broken axis（异常值或对数 0）\n278\t\n279\t**何时用**：数据范围跨度大，或对数轴需要表示 0。\n280\t\n281\t```python\n282\td = 0.012\n283\tkwargs = dict(transform=ax.transAxes, color='black', clip_on=False, linewidth=0.6)\n284\tax.plot((1-d, 1+d), (-d, +d), **kwargs)  # 右下角斜线\n285\tax.plot((1-d, 1+d), (1-d, 1+d), **kwargs)  # 右上角斜线\n286\t```\n287\t\n288\t### Pattern J：Path-effect 文字描边\n289\t\n290\t**何时用**：标注必须放在数据点 / colored region 上方。\n291\t\n292\t```python\n293\timport matplotlib.patheffects as pe\n294\tt = ax.text(x, y, 'label', fontsize=8, color='red')\n295\tt.set_path_effects([pe.withStroke(linewidth=2.5, foreground='white')])\n296\t```\n297\t\n298\t### Pattern K：Concept curve（建立直觉）\n299\t\n300\t**何时用**：论文核心 claim 是定性现象，需要先让读者理解理论预期。\n301\t\n302\t```python\n303\t# 完全合成的 cosine trajectory，不是实验数据\n304\tx = np.linspace(0, 1, 100)\n305\ttrajectory = baseline * (1 - np.cos(np.pi * x)) / 2\n306\tax.plot(x, trajectory, linewidth=2, solid_capstyle='round')\n307\tax.annotate('gap eliminated', (1.0, final_y), color='#388E3C',\n308\t            fontstyle='italic', fontsize=9)\n309\t```\n310\t\n311\t### Pattern L：Forest plot（meta-analysis 风格）\n312\t\n313\t**何时用**：多个子条件的 effect size + CI 对比，加 inline 统计数字。\n314\t\n315\t3-column GridSpec：(1) 标签 (2) CI 区间图 (3) 统计数字（mean, CI, p-value）\n316\t\n317\t每行：dot + horizontal line(CI) + 数字 inline。等价带用半透明 axvspan。\n318\t\n319\t### 概念图 L1 防丑原则（7 条）\n320\t\n321\t概念图（架构图、流程图、framework 示意）的视觉底线，与统计图 L1 互补。\n322\t\n323\t1. **Flow direction 一致** — 全图统一左→右或上→下，不混合方向\n324\t2. **Box 规格统一** — 同层级 box 尺寸、圆角、边框粗细一致\n325\t3. **箭头样式统一** — 不混用多种箭头类型（除非有语义区分）\n326\t4. **层级清晰** — 不同抽象层用缩进/嵌套/背景色区分\n327\t5. **密度控制** — 单张图 ≤15 个 box，超过则拆分或分组\n328\t6. **Label 完整** — 每个 box 和箭头都有文字说明\n329\t7. **无装饰性元素** — 不加纯装饰图标、渐变、3D 效果\n330\t\n331\t### 概念图 L2 叙事原则（6 条）\n332\t\n333\t让概念图不只是\"画了个框图\"，而是\"引导读者理解系统\"。\n334\t\n335\t1. **视觉锚点** — 核心贡献用饱和色/大字/加粗边框突出，其余 muted\n336\t2. **阅读路径** — 沿箭头方向自然读完全图，不需要跳跃\n337\t3. **对比布局** — 左右并列展示 before/after 或 baseline/ours\n338\t4. **分组嵌套** — 用浅色背景框将相关组件分组（如 encoder/decoder）\n339\t5. **箭头语义** — 实线=数据流，虚线=控制流/可选，粗线=关键路径\n340\t6. **信息层级** — 主路径用饱和色+大字，辅助信息用灰色+小字\n341\t\n342\t### 概念图 L3 Pattern 库\n343\t\n344\t| Pattern | 名称 | 适用场景 |\n345\t|---------|------|---------|\n346\t| P-A | 线性流程 | 数据处理 pipeline、推理流程 |\n347\t| P-B | 层级嵌套 | 模型架构（encoder/decoder/attention） |\n348\t| P-C | 对比并列 | baseline vs ours、before/after |\n349\t| P-D | 分支汇聚 | 多输入融合、ensemble |\n350\t| P-E | 循环 | 训练循环、迭代优化 |\n351\t| P-F | 诊断链 | 消融实验逻辑、排错流程 |\n352\t| P-G | 三面板综合 | 概念+数据+结论（常用于 Figure 1） |\n353\t\n354\t### 概念图 Checklist（7 项）\n355\t\n356\t- [ ] Flow direction 全图统一（左→右或上→下）\n357\t- [ ] Box 规格一致（同层级尺寸、圆角、边框相同）\n358\t- [ ] 视觉层级清晰（核心突出，辅助 muted）\n359\t- [ ] 核心贡献在视觉上最突出\n360\t- [ ] 箭头语义一致（实线/虚线/粗细有意义）\n361\t- [ ] 留白充足（box 之间不拥挤）\n362\t- [ ] 跨图风格一致（配色、字号、线宽与论文其他图统一）\n363\t\n364\t---\n365\t\n366\t## L4 工具路由 + 模板\n367\t\n368\t### 路由规则\n369\t\n370\t| 场景 | 工具 | 原因 |\n371\t|------|------|------|\n372\t| 概念图（架构图、流程图、framework 示意） | **PaperBanana（Gemini）** | matplotlib 手写 boxes/arrows 极痛苦 |\n373\t| 统计图（scatter、bar、line、heatmap、forest） | **matplotlib** | 确定性、可控、有模板 |\n374\t| 离线 / 无 GOOGLE_API_KEY | **matplotlib** | 不依赖外部 API |\n375\t\n376\t项目级偏好：`config/projects/{name}.yaml` 的 `visualization.preferred_tool` 可覆盖。\n377\t\n378\t### Matplotlib 通用样式（rcParams 标准）\n379\t\n380\t```python\n381\timport matplotlib\n382\tmatplotlib.use('Agg')\n383\timport matplotlib.pyplot as plt\n384\timport matplotlib.patheffects as pe\n385\timport numpy as np\n386\t\n387\tplt.rcParams.update({\n388\t    'font.family': 'DejaVu Sans',\n389\t    'font.size': 11, 'axes.titlesize': 13, 'axes.labelsize': 12,\n390\t    'xtick.labelsize': 10, 'ytick.labelsize': 10, 'legend.fontsize': 10,\n391\t    'figure.dpi': 300, 'savefig.dpi': 300,\n392\t    'savefig.bbox': 'tight', 'savefig.pad_inches': 0.05,\n393\t    'axes.spines.top': False, 'axes.spines.right': False,\n394\t    'pdf.fonttype': 42, 'ps.fonttype': 42,  # TrueType 嵌入\n395\t    'lines.linewidth': 1.8,\n396\t})\n397\t\n398\t# 色盲友好语义色板（Okabe-Ito）\n399\tSEMANTIC_COLORS = {\n400\t    'baseline':   '#0072B2',  # blue - safe / mild\n401\t    'treatment':  '#E69F00',  # orange - intervention\n402\t    'collapse':   '#D55E00',  # vermillion - danger / catastrophic\n403\t    'safe':       '#009E73',  # green - good outcome\n404\t    'neutral':    '#999999',  # gray - reference / control\n405\t}\n406\t```\n407\t\n408\t### 常用模板（仅作起点，需按 narrative_intent 改造）\n409\t\n410\t详细执行模板见 `skills/global/draw-paper-figure.skill.md`：\n411\t- Reliability Diagram（校准图）\n412\t- ECE Heatmap（方法 × 数据集 ΔECE）\n413\t- T* Scatter Plot（任务 Regime 可视化）\n414\t\n415\t**重要**：这些模板是起点不是终点。每张图根据 `narrative_intent` 必须加 L3 中的相关 pattern，不能直接套用。\n416\t\n417\t### 主图（Figure 1）特别提示\n418\t\n419\t大多数 ML 论文的 Figure 1 是概念图/架构图/方法概览——正是 PaperBanana 的强项。除非 Figure 1 是纯数值结果对比（如 Pareto frontier），否则优先用 PaperBanana。Figure 1 的 `narrative_intent` 必须与 `writing_plan.yaml` 的 `meta.core_claim` 直接关联。\n420\t\n421\t### PaperBanana 用法\n422\t\n423\t见 `skills/global/paper-banana.skill.md`。意图描述时直接引用 `narrative_intent`，并把 L3 中的 pattern 名写进 intent（如 \"with collapse zone background band, hollow markers for n<50\"）。\n424\t\n425\t---\n426\t\n427\t## 给 Paper Agent 的填写规则\n428\t\n429\t规划 `visualization_plan` 时，对每张图填：\n430\t\n431\t```yaml\n432\t- fig_id: fig2_main_result\n433\t  type: statistical\n434\t  tool: matplotlib\n435\t  data_source: \"registry: exp_001, exp_002\"\n436\t  placement: experiments\n437\t\n438\t  # 新字段（必填）\n439\t  narrative_intent: \"Quantized models match FP16 on accuracy but degrade calibration; ECE gap widens with bits→2.\"\n440\t  design_notes:\n441\t    - \"Pattern A: regime bands for bits=8/4/3\"\n442\t    - \"Pattern C: dashed line for FP16 ECE baseline\"\n443\t    - \"Pattern D: hollow markers for n<50 data points\"\n444\t\n445\t  aspect_ratio: \"4:3\"\n446\t```\n447\t\n448\t`narrative_intent`：一句话，主动语态，描述读者应该相信的事。\n449\t\n450\t`design_notes`：从 L3 选 0-4 个 pattern，给出选用理由。可以为空（说明默认模板足够）。\n451\t\n452\t---\n453\t\n454\t## 提交前 Checklist（以下为统计图专用）\n455\t\n456\tL1 部分（防丑底线）：\n457\t- [ ] 所有轴有 label + 单位\n458\t- [ ] 无 dual y-axis、无 3D、无 pie\n459\t- [ ] 颜色绑定语义、色盲友好\n460\t- [ ] Bar chart 从 0 起（如有）\n461\t- [ ] 字号 ≥ 10pt（缩到 column width 后仍可读）\n462\t- [ ] PDF 矢量、300 DPI\n463\t\n464\tL2 部分（叙事强度）：\n465\t- [ ] 能用一句话写出 figure takeaway（应等于 `narrative_intent`）\n466\t- [ ] Subtitle / panel label 是 insight，不是描述\n467\t- [ ] 数据质量差异在视觉上可见（n、OOS、pending 等）\n468\t- [ ] 至少考虑过是否需要 background band / fill_between / reference line（不一定要加）\n469\t\n470\tL3 部分（高级感——不可跳过）：\n471\t- [ ] 不像 matplotlib gallery 示例（无默认灰背景、无粗网格、无 tab10 色板）\n472\t- [ ] 配色 muted 和谐，不刺眼，不过度饱和\n473\t- [ ] 线宽、字号、间距全图统一\n474\t- [ ] 元素对齐严格（bars 底部对齐、labels 水平对齐）\n475\t- [ ] 留白充足（元素之间不拥挤）\n476\t- [ ] 与论文全文其他图的风格一致\n477\t- [ ] 终极判断：这张图放在 NeurIPS best paper 里违和吗？\n478\t"}]},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"367e5915-1d66-4be5-8527-39ffd8d65e90","uuid":"1fb1aacb-74e2-441a-a70a-03d2bd30e714","timestamp":"2026-05-24T07:34:48.011Z","tool_use_result":{"type":"text","file":{"filePath":"/home/ubuntu/agent-ml-research/skills/global/paper-visualization.skill.md","content":"---\ndescription: 学术论文图表设计与生成——四层指南（防丑原则 + 叙事原则 + Pattern 库 + 工具路由）。Paper Agent 规划 visualization_plan 时读，Worker 执行时也读。\nname: paper-visualization\nscope: global\ntags:\n- research\n- visualization\n- paper\n- matplotlib\n- paperbanana\nversion: 5\nroles:\n- agent\n- paper_agent\n---\n\n# Paper Visualization — 学术图表设计与生成\n\n> **核心理念**：好图不是\"画得对\"，是\"回答一个问题\"。每张图动笔前先问：**这张图要让读者看到什么？**\n>\n> **质量底线**：每张图必须达到 NeurIPS/ICML/EMNLP best paper 的视觉水准——高级感、专业感、精致感。绝不接受 matplotlib 默认样式的廉价感。\n\n本 skill 分五层：\n\n- **L0 学习同行** — 设计前看几张优秀同类型论文图，比读任何原则都有效\n- **L1 防丑原则** — 底线，所有图必须遵守\n- **L2 叙事原则** — 让图有说服力（区分\"数据图\"和\"论证图\"）\n- **L3 Pattern 库** — 具体技法目录，按 `design_notes` 选用\n- **L4 工具路由 + 模板** — paperbanana vs matplotlib，常用统计图模板\n\n---\n\n## L0 学习同行（设计前的功课）\n\n你的子领域里，**最被引用、最被记住的论文是怎么画图的？** 在动笔之前花点时间去看一眼——这个动作的回报远高于读完下面的 L1-L4。\n\n### 为什么这一步是重要的\n\n- 同领域读者已经被这些论文的视觉语言\"训练\"过——遵循惯例 = 降低读者认知负担\n- 顶会论文经历过 reviewer 严苛审查，能登出来的图基本说明它的 narrative 设计是被接受的\n- 抽象原则告诉你\"用 fill_between 表示发散\"，但好论文告诉你\"在这种数据上，发散到底应该画多淡才合适\"\n- 一个领域往往有它特有的图表惯例（如校准研究的 reliability diagram、scaling 研究的 log-log curve、RL 的 learning curve with seed bands），不看就不会用\n\n### 什么时候值得投入\n\n判断权交给你。一些可参考的信号：\n\n- **值得多花时间**：会决定论文成败的图（hero figure、main result、新颖的实验类型）\n- **不太值得**：常规 supplementary 图、loss curve 这类标准化已经很高的图\n- **强烈建议看**：你打算画一种自己不熟悉的图，或者数据形态是这个领域少见的\n\n### 怎么看\n\n工具：`paper-image-extractor` skill 可以从 arXiv source 或 PDF 提取参考论文里的 figure。你已经有 `literature_map.yaml` 里的论文清单作为起点。\n\n看的时候问自己（不是机械检查清单，是真的去理解）：\n\n- 这张图回答了什么问题？读者一眼能 get 到吗？\n- 它用了哪些叙事层（background band / 参考线 / 概念曲线 / fill 区域）？这些层在我的数据上有对应物吗？\n- 它的颜色和 marker 怎么承载语义？我的图能不能借鉴这套映射？\n- 它有什么是这篇论文特有的（与他们的具体数据 / claim 绑死）？这些不要照搬。\n- 如果让我重画它的图，我会怎么改？为什么作者没这么做？\n\n### 怎么把学到的传下去\n\n把你看完后的判断**直接写进对应 fig 的 `design_notes`**，用人话不用模板。例子：\n\n```yaml\ndesign_notes:\n  - \"学 GPTQ Fig 4：用 log-scale bit-width + dashed FP16 baseline + 不同 marker shape 分模型族\"\n  - \"Pattern A（regime band）划分 in-domain / OOD 两段\"\n  - \"AWQ Fig 2 那种 hatching 区分 outlier 不适用我们的数据，跳过\"\n```\n\nWorker 拿到这种 design_notes 就知道既要参考 GPTQ 的 layout 又要用 Pattern A，能直接落实成代码。\n\n### 边界\n\n- 不要复刻，要消化。直接抄一张图既没用（数据不一样）也有风险（与他们的 claim 强绑定）\n- 不要看太多。3 张就够，多了 Paper Agent 会陷入\"该听谁的\"\n- 看不到原图（提取失败 / arXiv 无 source）就跳过，不要在这里卡住流程\n\n---\n\n## L1 防丑原则（底线，不可违反）\n\n来自 Tufte 和 Luís Cruz 16 条公认规范。Agent 默认遵守，无需逐条思考。\n\n1. **Self-explanatory** — 不看正文就能看懂：title/caption + 所有轴有 label + 单位\n2. **去 chartjunk** — 不要 3D、不要背景框、不要无意义网格、不要 dual y-axis、不要 pie/donut chart\n3. **直接标注 > Legend** — 能直接标在曲线/柱旁就不用 legend；legend 必要时不遮数据\n4. **颜色/形状/纹理只用于 data variation** — 不用颜色装饰，不用相同颜色表示不同数据\n5. **Meaningful baseline** — bar chart 必须从 0 起；scatter 可以非 0\n6. **文字水平** — label 不要旋转/垂直；类别名太长 → 用 horizontal bar chart\n7. **Sort for comparison** — 柱状图按数值排序，便于对比（除非有自然顺序如时间）\n8. **字号一致** — 用 mm 计算 figsize（`ONE_MM = 1/25.4`），不在 LaTeX 里 `\\includegraphics[scale=...]` resize\n9. **色盲友好** — 默认 Okabe-Ito；红/绿区分时变 saturation；考虑 grayscale 打印\n10. **Bold 只用于强调** — 不要全图加粗；highlight 留给 takeaway\n11. **Line plot 只用于有序数据** — 类别变量用 bar\n12. **单 x 单 y 轴** — 必须双轴时改成 multi-panel\n13. **300 DPI、PDF 矢量、savefig.bbox=tight、去掉 top/right spines**\n\n---\n\n## L2 叙事原则（让图变成论证）\n\nL1 让图\"不丑\"，L2 让图\"有说服力\"。这一层是 Agent 自主画图时最容易缺失的。\n\n### 原则 1：每张图回答一个明确问题\n\n动笔前用一句话写下：\"This figure shows that ___.\"\n\n如果写不出来，就不要画。如果写出来的 takeaway 不是论文的 claim，就要重新设计。\n\n**反例**：`\"Accuracy across methods\"` — 这只是个标题，不是问题\n**正例**：`\"FP16 baseline 在小模型上比量化版高 5pp，但 70B 模型上差距消失\"` — 这是一张图要说服读者相信的事\n\n`visualization_plan.{fig_id}.narrative_intent` 字段写这一句。\n\n### 原则 2：颜色绑定语义而非数据顺序\n\n错的做法：`colors = ['blue', 'orange', 'green', 'red']` 按数据顺序分配。\n对的做法：`BN → 蓝色（始终）`、`non-BN → 橙色（始终）`、`collapse/danger → vermillion (#D55E00)`、`safe/baseline → blue (#0072B2)`。\n\n整篇论文 6 张图，BN 永远蓝、collapse 永远红——零认知负担，读者一眼建立映射。\n\n### 原则 3：信息层级用 visual variable 表达\n\n数据的\"质量\"和\"分类\"应该可见，不应该藏在 caption 里：\n\n| 信息 | Visual 表达 |\n|------|------------|\n| n < 50 低置信度点 | hollow marker + 标注 `n=` |\n| Out-of-sample / pending 数据 | hollow + 不同 marker shape（如 diamond）+ \"OOS\" label |\n| Collapse / 异常类别 | hatching（斜线填充）|\n| 主结果 vs 次要结果 | 主用饱和色，次要用 muted 色或 alpha |\n\n### 原则 4：加叙事层，不只画数据\n\n数据是图的基础，叙事层是让读者\"读懂\"的关键。常见叙事层：\n\n- **Background bands** — `axhspan/axvspan` 标定性区域（\"collapse zone\"、\"safe zone\"、\"Regime A/B/C\"）\n- **Fill_between** — 显示两条曲线发散区域（alpha 极低如 0.06，不抢眼但有面积）\n- **Reference lines** — 锚定真实值（如 dashed line 标\"actual W3A16 accuracy\"）\n- **Equivalence band** — `axvspan` gradient fade 标 +/-1pp 等价区间\n- **Concept curve** — 完全合成的曲线（如 cosine trajectory）建立理论直觉\n\n这些都不来自数据，但帮读者\"看到\"数据想说什么。\n\n### 原则 5：Subtitle / panel label 写 insight 不写描述\n\n错：`\"Layer 6 comparison across three models\"`\n对：`\"Same relative position, opposite function\"`\n\n错：`\"Reliability diagram\"`\n对：`\"Quantization shifts confidence but not accuracy\"`\n\n副标题是免费的 takeaway，不要浪费。\n\n### 原则 6：Multi-panel 每个面板独立回答一个 sub-question\n\n不要做\"主图 + 装饰小图\"。每个 panel 必须有独立的信息价值。\n\n如果一个 panel 想不出独立的问题，要么删掉，要么合并到主 panel。\n\n布局上：非对称比例（如 1.4:1）让视觉重心对齐叙事重心。\n\n### 原则 7：所有图至少 3 轮迭代，Hero figure 至少 5 轮\n\n第一版画出来基本不能直接用。计划好预算：先确定数据和布局，再调样式标注，最后 polish。不要指望一次到位。\n\n### Hero Figure 定义\n\nHero Figure = Figure 1 + 主结果图（main result figure）。这两类图对论文第一印象影响最大，必须投入更多迭代。\n\n---\n\n## L3 Pattern 库（按 `design_notes` 选用）\n\n按\"图要回答的问题\"选 pattern。每个 pattern 给出**何时用**、**怎么用**（具体代码或 matplotlib API）。\n\n### Pattern A：定性区域 / regime bands\n\n**何时用**：x 或 y 轴有自然分段（regime、phase、severity tier）。\n\n```python\n# horizontal bands\nfor lo, hi, color, label in [(0, 1, '#EBF5FB', 'Regime A'), (1, 5, '#EAFAF1', 'Regime B')]:\n    ax.axhspan(lo, hi, alpha=0.25, color=color, zorder=0)\n    ax.text(x_label_pos, (lo+hi)/2, label, va='center', fontsize=9, color='gray')\n\n# 单一 collapse zone\nax.axhspan(-5, 5, color='#FDEDEC', alpha=0.5, zorder=0)\nax.text(0.02, 2.5, 'collapse zone', fontsize=8, color='#ccaaaa', fontstyle='italic')\n```\n\n### Pattern B：发散面积 fill_between\n\n**何时用**：两组数据从某点开始发散，想让\"发散程度\"可见。\n\n```python\n# alpha 0.06 极淡，不抢眼但有面积\nax.fill_between(x_range, curve_high, curve_low, alpha=0.06, color='#555555', zorder=1)\n```\n\n### Pattern C：参考线锚定真实值\n\n**何时用**：合成 / 缩放后的 x 轴需要锚定到真实物理量。\n\n```python\nax.axhline(y=actual_value, color=color, linestyle=':', linewidth=0.8, alpha=0.3)\nt = ax.text(x_pos, actual_value+1, 'actual W3A16', fontsize=7, color=color, alpha=0.5)\nt.set_path_effects([pe.withStroke(linewidth=2.5, foreground='white')])  # 防遮挡\n```\n\n### Pattern D：信息层级 marker（hollow vs solid）\n\n**何时用**：同一图中有不同置信度 / OOS / pending 数据。\n\n```python\n# 实心 = n >= 50, 空心 = n < 50\nif n < 50:\n    ax.plot(x, y, marker='o', markerfacecolor='white', markeredgecolor=color,\n            markeredgewidth=1.2, markersize=6)\n    ax.annotate(f'n={n}', (x, y), xytext=(0, 7), textcoords='offset points',\n                fontsize=5, color=color, fontstyle='italic')\n```\n\n### Pattern E：Wilson CI / bootstrap 不确定性\n\n**何时用**：accuracy / proportion 数据需要置信区间。\n\n```python\ndef wilson_ci(p, n, z=1.96):\n    denom = 1 + z**2 / n\n    center = (p + z**2 / (2*n)) / denom\n    half = z * np.sqrt((p*(1-p) + z**2/(4*n)) / n) / denom\n    return max(0, center - half), min(1, center + half)\n```\n\n### Pattern F：Per-label offset dict（解决标注重叠）\n\n**何时用**：scatter 上多个点需要标注且自动布局重叠。\n\n```python\nlabel_offsets = {\n    'Qwen3-8B':  (6, -12),   # 向下避让\n    'Qwen3-4B':  (-55, 4),   # 向左避让\n    'Llama-8B':  (6, 6),\n}\nfor name, (x, y) in data.items():\n    ox, oy = label_offsets.get(name, (6, 6))\n    ax.annotate(name, (x, y), xytext=(ox, oy), textcoords='offset points', fontsize=7)\n```\n\n### Pattern G：Hatching 加分类维度\n\n**何时用**：已用颜色表示一个维度，再加一个二元维度（如 collapse vs not）。\n\n```python\nfor is_collapse, bar in zip(collapse_flags, bars):\n    if is_collapse:\n        bar.set_hatch('///')\n        bar.set_alpha(0.7)\n```\n\n### Pattern H：Multi-panel 非对称布局\n\n**何时用**：主图 + 辅助 panel；或两个等重要 panel 但视觉重心需要倾斜。\n\n```python\nfig, axes = plt.subplots(1, 2, figsize=(6.5, 3.2),\n                          gridspec_kw={'width_ratios': [1.4, 1]})\n# 主面板 1.4x 宽，副面板 1x；视觉重心在左\n```\n\n### Pattern I：Broken axis（异常值或对数 0）\n\n**何时用**：数据范围跨度大，或对数轴需要表示 0。\n\n```python\nd = 0.012\nkwargs = dict(transform=ax.transAxes, color='black', clip_on=False, linewidth=0.6)\nax.plot((1-d, 1+d), (-d, +d), **kwargs)  # 右下角斜线\nax.plot((1-d, 1+d), (1-d, 1+d), **kwargs)  # 右上角斜线\n```\n\n### Pattern J：Path-effect 文字描边\n\n**何时用**：标注必须放在数据点 / colored region 上方。\n\n```python\nimport matplotlib.patheffects as pe\nt = ax.text(x, y, 'label', fontsize=8, color='red')\nt.set_path_effects([pe.withStroke(linewidth=2.5, foreground='white')])\n```\n\n### Pattern K：Concept curve（建立直觉）\n\n**何时用**：论文核心 claim 是定性现象，需要先让读者理解理论预期。\n\n```python\n# 完全合成的 cosine trajectory，不是实验数据\nx = np.linspace(0, 1, 100)\ntrajectory = baseline * (1 - np.cos(np.pi * x)) / 2\nax.plot(x, trajectory, linewidth=2, solid_capstyle='round')\nax.annotate('gap eliminated', (1.0, final_y), color='#388E3C',\n            fontstyle='italic', fontsize=9)\n```\n\n### Pattern L：Forest plot（meta-analysis 风格）\n\n**何时用**：多个子条件的 effect size + CI 对比，加 inline 统计数字。\n\n3-column GridSpec：(1) 标签 (2) CI 区间图 (3) 统计数字（mean, CI, p-value）\n\n每行：dot + horizontal line(CI) + 数字 inline。等价带用半透明 axvspan。\n\n### 概念图 L1 防丑原则（7 条）\n\n概念图（架构图、流程图、framework 示意）的视觉底线，与统计图 L1 互补。\n\n1. **Flow direction 一致** — 全图统一左→右或上→下，不混合方向\n2. **Box 规格统一** — 同层级 box 尺寸、圆角、边框粗细一致\n3. **箭头样式统一** — 不混用多种箭头类型（除非有语义区分）\n4. **层级清晰** — 不同抽象层用缩进/嵌套/背景色区分\n5. **密度控制** — 单张图 ≤15 个 box，超过则拆分或分组\n6. **Label 完整** — 每个 box 和箭头都有文字说明\n7. **无装饰性元素** — 不加纯装饰图标、渐变、3D 效果\n\n### 概念图 L2 叙事原则（6 条）\n\n让概念图不只是\"画了个框图\"，而是\"引导读者理解系统\"。\n\n1. **视觉锚点** — 核心贡献用饱和色/大字/加粗边框突出，其余 muted\n2. **阅读路径** — 沿箭头方向自然读完全图，不需要跳跃\n3. **对比布局** — 左右并列展示 before/after 或 baseline/ours\n4. **分组嵌套** — 用浅色背景框将相关组件分组（如 encoder/decoder）\n5. **箭头语义** — 实线=数据流，虚线=控制流/可选，粗线=关键路径\n6. **信息层级** — 主路径用饱和色+大字，辅助信息用灰色+小字\n\n### 概念图 L3 Pattern 库\n\n| Pattern | 名称 | 适用场景 |\n|---------|------|---------|\n| P-A | 线性流程 | 数据处理 pipeline、推理流程 |\n| P-B | 层级嵌套 | 模型架构（encoder/decoder/attention） |\n| P-C | 对比并列 | baseline vs ours、before/after |\n| P-D | 分支汇聚 | 多输入融合、ensemble |\n| P-E | 循环 | 训练循环、迭代优化 |\n| P-F | 诊断链 | 消融实验逻辑、排错流程 |\n| P-G | 三面板综合 | 概念+数据+结论（常用于 Figure 1） |\n\n### 概念图 Checklist（7 项）\n\n- [ ] Flow direction 全图统一（左→右或上→下）\n- [ ] Box 规格一致（同层级尺寸、圆角、边框相同）\n- [ ] 视觉层级清晰（核心突出，辅助 muted）\n- [ ] 核心贡献在视觉上最突出\n- [ ] 箭头语义一致（实线/虚线/粗细有意义）\n- [ ] 留白充足（box 之间不拥挤）\n- [ ] 跨图风格一致（配色、字号、线宽与论文其他图统一）\n\n---\n\n## L4 工具路由 + 模板\n\n### 路由规则\n\n| 场景 | 工具 | 原因 |\n|------|------|------|\n| 概念图（架构图、流程图、framework 示意） | **PaperBanana（Gemini）** | matplotlib 手写 boxes/arrows 极痛苦 |\n| 统计图（scatter、bar、line、heatmap、forest） | **matplotlib** | 确定性、可控、有模板 |\n| 离线 / 无 GOOGLE_API_KEY | **matplotlib** | 不依赖外部 API |\n\n项目级偏好：`config/projects/{name}.yaml` 的 `visualization.preferred_tool` 可覆盖。\n\n### Matplotlib 通用样式（rcParams 标准）\n\n```python\nimport matplotlib\nmatplotlib.use('Agg')\nimport matplotlib.pyplot as plt\nimport matplotlib.patheffects as pe\nimport numpy as np\n\nplt.rcParams.update({\n    'font.family': 'DejaVu Sans',\n    'font.size': 11, 'axes.titlesize': 13, 'axes.labelsize': 12,\n    'xtick.labelsize': 10, 'ytick.labelsize': 10, 'legend.fontsize': 10,\n    'figure.dpi': 300, 'savefig.dpi': 300,\n    'savefig.bbox': 'tight', 'savefig.pad_inches': 0.05,\n    'axes.spines.top': False, 'axes.spines.right': False,\n    'pdf.fonttype': 42, 'ps.fonttype': 42,  # TrueType 嵌入\n    'lines.linewidth': 1.8,\n})\n\n# 色盲友好语义色板（Okabe-Ito）\nSEMANTIC_COLORS = {\n    'baseline':   '#0072B2',  # blue - safe / mild\n    'treatment':  '#E69F00',  # orange - intervention\n    'collapse':   '#D55E00',  # vermillion - danger / catastrophic\n    'safe':       '#009E73',  # green - good outcome\n    'neutral':    '#999999',  # gray - reference / control\n}\n```\n\n### 常用模板（仅作起点，需按 narrative_intent 改造）\n\n详细执行模板见 `skills/global/draw-paper-figure.skill.md`：\n- Reliability Diagram（校准图）\n- ECE Heatmap（方法 × 数据集 ΔECE）\n- T* Scatter Plot（任务 Regime 可视化）\n\n**重要**：这些模板是起点不是终点。每张图根据 `narrative_intent` 必须加 L3 中的相关 pattern，不能直接套用。\n\n### 主图（Figure 1）特别提示\n\n大多数 ML 论文的 Figure 1 是概念图/架构图/方法概览——正是 PaperBanana 的强项。除非 Figure 1 是纯数值结果对比（如 Pareto frontier），否则优先用 PaperBanana。Figure 1 的 `narrative_intent` 必须与 `writing_plan.yaml` 的 `meta.core_claim` 直接关联。\n\n### PaperBanana 用法\n\n见 `skills/global/paper-banana.skill.md`。意图描述时直接引用 `narrative_intent`，并把 L3 中的 pattern 名写进 intent（如 \"with collapse zone background band, hollow markers for n<50\"）。\n\n---\n\n## 给 Paper Agent 的填写规则\n\n规划 `visualization_plan` 时，对每张图填：\n\n```yaml\n- fig_id: fig2_main_result\n  type: statistical\n  tool: matplotlib\n  data_source: \"registry: exp_001, exp_002\"\n  placement: experiments\n\n  # 新字段（必填）\n  narrative_intent: \"Quantized models match FP16 on accuracy but degrade calibration; ECE gap widens with bits→2.\"\n  design_notes:\n    - \"Pattern A: regime bands for bits=8/4/3\"\n Agent requests usage\n4\tvia Feishu approval card — value is returned only after user approves.\n5\t\n6\tStorage layout:\n7\t  config/secrets/{project}/manifest.yaml   — key metadata (no values)\n8\t  config/secrets/{project}/{key_id}.enc    — encrypted key value\n9\t\"\"\"\n10\t\n11\tfrom __future__ import annotations\n12\t\n13\timport logging\n14\tfrom datetime import datetime, timezone\n15\tfrom pathlib import Path\n16\t\n17\timport yaml\n18\t\n19\tfrom core import paths as _paths\n20\tfrom core.crypto import save_secret, load_secret\n21\tfrom core.tools.result import ToolResult\n22\t\n23\tlogger = logging.getLogger(__name__)\n24\t\n25\t\n26\tdef _secrets_dir(project: str) -> Path:\n27\t    return _paths.config_dir() / \"secrets\" / project\n28\t\n29\t\n30\tdef _manifest_path(project: str) -> Path:\n31\t    return _secrets_dir(project) / \"manifest.yaml\"\n32\t\n33\t\n34\tdef _load_manifest(project: str) -> dict:\n35\t    path = _manifest_path(project)\n36\t    if not path.exists():\n37\t        return {}\n38\t    with open(path) as f:\n39\t        return yaml.safe_load(f) or {}\n40\t\n41\t\n42\tdef _save_manifest(project: str, data: dict) -> None:\n43\t    path = _manifest_path(project)\n44\t    path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)\n45\t    with open(path, \"w\") as f:\n46\t        yaml.dump(data, f, allow_unicode=True, default_flow_style=False)\n47\t\n48\t\n49\t# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n50\t# Public API\n51\t# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n52\t\n53\tdef store_secret(\n54\t    project: str,\n55\t    key_id: str,\n56\t    value: str,\n57\t    provider: str = only)."}]},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"367e5915-1d66-4be5-8527-39ffd8d65e90","uuid":"b392b57e-04e3-4631-84f0-7069c5349431","timestamp":"2026-05-24T07:35:53.522Z","tool_use_result":{"type":"text","file":{"filePath":"/home/ubuntu/agent-ml-research/core/tools/secrets.py","content":"\"\"\"Project secrets vault — encrypted per-project key storage with usage approval.\n\nDirector stores secrets (API keys, tokens, passwords). Agent requests usage\nvia Feishu approval card — value is returned only after user approves.\n\nStorage layout:\n  config/secrets/{project}/manifest.yaml   — key metadata (no values)\n  config/secrets/{project}/{key_id}.enc    — encrypted key value\n\"\"\"\n\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport logging\nfrom datetime import datetime, timezone\nfrom pathlib import Path\n\nimport yaml\n\nfrom core import paths as _paths\nfrom core.crypto import save_secret, load_secret\nfrom core.tools.result import ToolResult\n\nlogger = logging.getLogger(__name__)\n\n\ndef _secrets_dir(project: str) -> Path:\n    return _paths.config_dir() / \"secrets\" / project\n\n\ndef _manifest_path(project: str) -> Path:\n    return _secrets_dir(project) / \"manifest.yaml\"\n\n\ndef _load_manifest(project: str) -> dict:\n    path = _manifest_path(project)\n    if not path.exists():\n        return {}\n    with open(path) as f:\n        return yaml.safe_load(f) or {}\n\n\ndef _save_manifest(project: str, data: dict) -> None:\n    path = _manifest_path(project)\n    path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)\n    with open(path, \"w\") as f:\n        yaml.dump(data, f, allow_unicode=True, default_flow_style=False)\n\n\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n# Public API\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\ndef store_secret(\n    project: str,\n    key_id: str,\n    value: str,\n    provider: str = \"\",\n    purpose: str = \"\",\n) -> ToolResult:\n    \"\"\"Store an encrypted secret for a project (Director only).","numLines":60,"startLine":1,"totalLines":154}}}

(light yellow)\n    'BL': '#f8d7da',  # Entangling + Simple → mixed (light pink)\n    'BR': '#f5c6cb',  # Entangling + Complex → severe (pink)\n}\n\nquads = [\n    ('TL', 0.0, y_mid, x_mid, 1.0),\n x0 - 2*pad, y1 - y0 - 2*pad,\n                           boxstyle=\"round,pad=0.01\", facecolor=quadrant_colors[qid],\n                           edgecolor='#aaaaaa', linewidth=0.8, zorder=0)\n    ax_a.add_patch(rect)\n\n# Axis labels\nax_a.text(0.5, -0.06, 'Task Complexity', fontsize=12, ha='center', va='top',\n          fontweight='bold')\nax_a.annotate('', xy=(0.85, -0.02), xytext=(0.15, -0.02),\n              arrowprops=dict(arrowstyle='->', color='#555555', lw=1.2))\nax_a.text(0.18, -0.02, 'Simple', fontsize=9, ha='left', va='bottom', color='#555555')\nax_a.text(0.82, -0.02, 'Complex', fontsize=9, ha='right', va='bottom', color='#555555')\n\nax_a.text(-0.07, 0.5, 'Token Coupling', fontsize=12, ha='center', va='center',\n          fontweight='bold', rotation=90)\nax_a.annotate('', xy=(-0.03, 0.15), xytext=(-0.03, 0.85),\n              arrowprops=dict(arrowstyle='->', color='#555555', lw=1.2))\nax_a.text(-0.03, 0.82, 'Independent', fontsize=9, ha='right', va='top',\n          color='#555555', rotation=90)\nax_a.text(-0.03, 0.18, 'Entangling', fontsize=9, ha='right', va='bottom',\n          color='#555555', rotation=90)\n\n# Quadrant content\nstroke = [pe.withStroke(linewidth=2.5, foreground='white')]\n\n# TL: Independent + Simple\nax_a.text(0.25, 0.90, 'Object Suite', fontsize=10, ha='center', va='center',\n          fontweight='bold', color='#333333')\n# pi0.5\nax_a.text(0.25, 0.81, r'$\\pi$0.5: 98.2% $\\rightarrow$ 98.2%', fontsize=9,\n          ha='center', va='center', color=C_SAFE)\nax_a.text(0.25, 0.73, '(0.0pp drop at k=0.9)', fontsize=8,\n          ha='center', va='center', color=C_SAFE, fontstyle='italic')\n# OpenVLA\nax_a.text(0.25, 0.63, 'OpenVLA: 74% $\\\\rightarrow$ 62%', fontsize=9,\n          ha='center', va='center', color=C_MILD)\nax_a.text(0.25, 0.56, '(−12pp at k=0.9)', fontsize=8,\n          ha='center', va='center', color=C_MILD, fontstyle='italic')\n\n# TR: Independent + Complex\nax_a.text(0.75, 0.90, 'Goal Suite', fontsize=10, ha='center', va='center',\n          fontweight='bold', color='#333333')\nax_a.text(0.75, 0.79, r'$\\pi$0.5: 98.2% $\\rightarrow$ −7.0pp', fontsize=9,\n          ha='center', va='center', color=C_MILD)\nax_a.text(0.75, 0.71, '(at k=0.95)', fontsize=8,\n          ha='center', va='center', color=C_MILD, fontstyle='italic')\n\n# BL: Entangling + Simple\nax_a.text(0.25, 0.40, 'Object Suite', fontsize=10, ha='center', va='center',\n          fontweight='bold', color='#333333')\n# X-VLA\nax_a.text(0.25, 0.31, 'X-VLA: 99.2% $\\\\rightarrow$ 97.6%', fontsize=9,\n          ha='center', va='center', color=C_SAFE)\nax_a.text(0.25, 0.24, '(−1.6pp at k=0.9)', fontsize=8,\n          ha='center', va='center', color=C_SAFE, fontstyle='italic')\n# SmolVLA\nax_a.text(0.25, 0.14, 'SmolVLA: 59.2% $\\\\rightarrow$ 0%', fontsize=9,\n          ha='center', va='center', color=C_SEVERE)\nax_a.text(0.25, 0.07, '(collapse at k=0.95)', fontsize=8,\n          ha='center', va='center', color=C_SEVERE, fontstyle='italic')\n\n# BR: Entangling + Complex\nax_a.text(0.75, 0.40, 'Goal Suite', fontsize=10, ha='center', va='center',\n          fontweight='bold', color='#333333')\n# X-VLA\nax_a.text(0.75, 0.31, 'X-VLA: 98% $\\\\rightarrow$ 70.2%', fontsize=9,\n          ha='center', va='center', color=C_DANGER)\nax_a.text(0.75, 0.24, '(−27.8pp at k=0.9)', fontsize=8,\n          ha='center', va='center', color=C_DANGER, fontstyle='italic',\n          fontweight='bold')\n# SmolVLA\nax_a.text(0.75, 0.14, 'SmolVLA: 0% at any k', fontsize=9,\n          ha='center', va='center', color=C_SEVERE)\nax_a.text(0.75, 0.07, '(total collapse)', fontsize=8,\n          ha='center', va='center', color=C_SEVERE, fontstyle='italic')\n\n# Method note\nax_a.text(0.5, 1.04, 'L2-norm pruning', fontsize=9, ha='center', va='bottom',\n          color='#777777', fontstyle='italic')\n\n# Divider lines\nax_a.plot([x_mid, x_mid], [0.02, 0.98], color='#aaaaaa', linewidth=0.8,\n          linestyle='--', zorder=1)\nax_a.plot([0.02, 0.98], [y_mid, y_mid], color='#aaaaaa', linewidth=0.8,\n          linestyle='--', zorder=1)\n\n# ============================================================\n# Panel (b): X-VLA pruning curves\n# ============================================================\nax_b = fig.add_subplot(gs[1])\n\nax_b.text(-0.12, 1.06, '(b)', fontsize=14, fontweight='bold',\n          transform=ax_b.transAxes, va='top', ha='left')\n\n# Data\nk_object = [1.0, 0.95, 0.9, 0.85, 0.8, 0.7, 0.5, 0.3]\nsr_object = [99.2, 99.0, 97.6, 68.0, 89.0, 56.2, 42.6, 23.0]\n\nk_goal = [1.0, 0.9, 0.5]\nsr_goal = [98.0, 70.2, 61.0]\n\n# Plot Object suite\nax_b.plot(k_object, sr_object, 'o-', color=C_BLUE, markersize=7,\n          label='Object suite', zorder=3, markeredgecolor='white', markeredgewidth=0.8)\n\n# Plot Goal suite\nax_b.plot(k_goal, sr_goal, 's-', color=C_RED, markersize=8,\n          label='Goal suite', zorder=3, markeredgecolor='white', markeredgewidth=0.8)\n\n# Baseline dashed lines\nax_b.axhline(y=99.2, color=C_BLUE, linestyle=':', linewidth=0.9, alpha=0.4, zorder=1)\nax_b.axhline(y=98.0, color=C_RED, linestyle=':', linewidth=0.9, alpha=0.4, zorder=1)\n\n# Annotate key points with path effects\ndef annotate_pt(ax, text, xy, xytext, color, fontsize=9, bold=False):\n    t = ax.annotate(text, xy=xy, xytext=xytext,\n                    textcoords='offset points', fontsize=fontsize,\n                    color=color, fontweight='bold' if bold else 'normal',\n                    arrowprops=dict(arrowstyle='-', color=color, lw=0.6, alpha=0.5),\n                    zorder=5)\n    t.set_path_effects([pe.withStroke(linewidth=2.5, foreground='white')])\n    return t\n\n# Direct labels near k=0.9 points — no connecting arrows, just text\nax_b.text(0.9, 100.5, '97.6%', fontsize=9, color=C_BLUE, ha='center', va='bottom',\n          path_effects=[pe.withStroke(linewidth=2.5, foreground='white')])\nax_b.text(0.9, 66.5, '70.2%', fontsize=9.5, color=C_RED, ha='center', va='top',\n          fontweight='bold',\n          path_effects=[pe.withStroke(linewidth=2.5, foreground='white')])\n\n# Vertical double-arrow at k=0.895 (slightly offset to avoid marker overlap)\nax_b.annotate('', xy=(0.895, 95.5), xytext=(0.895, 72.5),\n              arrowprops=dict(arrowstyle='<->', color='#333333', lw=1.5),\n              zorder=4)\n\n# \"27.8pp gap\" label — placed to the right of the arrow (lower k region)\nax_b.text(0.845, 84, '27.8pp\\ngap', fontsize=10, ha='right', va='center',\n          color='#333333', fontweight='bold', linespacing=1.2,\n          path_effects=[pe.withStroke(linewidth=3, foreground='white')])\n\n# Light shaded band at k=0.9\nax_b.axvspan(0.888, 0.912, alpha=0.07, color='#555555', zorder=0)\n\n# Axis config\nax_b.set_xlabel('Keep ratio $k$', fontsize=12)\nax_b.set_ylabel('Success Rate (%)', fontsize=12)\nax_b.set_xlim(0.25, 1.07)\nax_b.set_ylim(15, 105)\nax_b.set_xticks([0.3, 0.5, 0.7, 0.8, 0.9, 1.0])\nax_b.set_xticklabels(['0.3', '0.5', '0.7', '0.8', '0.9', '1.0'])\nax_b.invert_xaxis()\n\nax_b.set_title('X-VLA (L2-norm pruning): same model, same $k$,\\ndramatic task-dependent gap',\n               fontsize=10, fontstyle='italic', color='#444444', pad=8)\n\n# Direct curve labels near the curves\nax_b.text(0.42, 38, 'Object', fontsize=10, color=C_BLUE, fontweight='bold',\n          path_effects=[pe.withStroke(linewidth=2.5, foreground='white')])\nax_b.text(0.42, 64, 'Goal', fontsize=10, color=C_RED, fontweight='bold',\n          path_effects=[pe.withStroke(linewidth=2.5, foreground='white')])\n\n# ============================================================\n# Save\n# ============================================================\noutdir = '/home/ubuntu/.agent-ml-research-be-idea-gen-0515-2/projects/importance_margin_vla_compress/docs/paper/figures/paper'\nfig.savefig(f'{outdir}/fig_teaser.pdf')\nfig.savefig(f'{outdir}/fig_teaser.png')\nprint(f'Saved fig_teaser.pdf and fig_teaser.png')\n","numLines":239,"startLine":1,"totalLines":239}}}

{"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-6","id":"msg_01NBVz6JPUxESoBAbrCL4Sv2","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_01H9NSuyeECt5rspVLS7kmzA","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"/home/ubuntu/.agent-ml-research-be-idea-gen-0515-2/projects/importance_margin_vla_compress/docs/paper/figures/paper/gen_fig_hero_overview.py","content":"import matplotlib\nmatplotlib.use('Agg')\nimport matplotlib.pyplot as plt\nimport matplotlib.patches as mpatches\nfrom matplotlib.patches import FancyBboxPatch, FancyArrowPatch\nimport matplotlib.patheffects as pe\nimport numpy as np\n\nplt.rcParams.update({\n    'font.family': 'DejaVu Sans',\n    'font.size': 10,\n    'figure.dpi': 300,\n    'savefig.dpi': 300,\n    'savefig.bbox': 'tight',\n    'savefig.pad_inches': 0.08,\n    'pdf.fonttype': 42,\n    'ps.fonttype': 42,\n})\n\nOUTDIR = '/home/ubuntu/.agent-ml-research-be-idea-gen-0515-2/projects/importance_margin_vla_compress/docs/paper/figures/paper'\n\n# Colors\nC_GREEN_BG = '#D5F5E3'\nC_GREEN_BD = '#27AE60'\nC_GREEN_TXT = '#1E8449'\nC_YELLOW_BG = '#FCF3CF'\nC_YELLOW_BD = '#F39C12'\nC_YELLOW_TXT = '#B7950B'\nC_ORANGE_BG = '#FDEBD0'\nC_ORANGE_BD = '#E67E22'\nC_ORANGE_TXT = '#CA6F1E'\nC_RED_BG = '#FADBD8'\nC_RED_BD = '#E74C3C'\nC_RED_TXT = '#C0392B'\nC_BLUE_BG = '#D6EAF8'\nC_BLUE_BD = '#3498DB'\nC_DARK = '#2C3E50'\nC_GRAY = '#7F8C8D'\nC_LGRAY = '#BDC3C7'\n\nSHADOW_PROPS = [pe.withSimplePatchShadow(offset=(1.5, -1.5), shadow_rgbFace='#cccccc', alpha=0.12)]\n\n\ndef add_rounded_box(ax, xy, width, height, facecolor, edgecolor, linewidth=0.6, text=None,\n                    subtitle=None, fontsize=11, sub_fontsize=8, text_color=C_DARK,\n                    zorder=2, pad=0.015, shadow=True):\n    x, y = xy\n    box = FancyBboxPatch((x, y), width, height,\n                         boxstyle=f\"round,pad={pad}\",\n                         facecolor=facecolor, edgecolor=edgecolor,\n                         linewidth=linewidth, zorder=zorder,\n                         mutation_aspect=1.0)\n    if shadow:\n        box.set_path_effects(SHADOW_PROPS)\n    ax.add_patch(box)\n    if text:\n        ax.text(x + width / 2, y + height * 0.6, text,\n                fontsize=fontsize, fontweight='bold', ha='center', va='center',\n                color=text_color, zorder=zorder + 1)\n    if subtitle:\n        ax.text(x + width / 2, y + height * 0.25, subtitle,\n                fontsize=sub_fontsize, ha='center', va='center',\n                color=C_GRAY, fontstyle='italic', zorder=zorder + 1)\n    return box\n\n\ndef draw_arrow(ax, start, end, color='#555555', lw=0.8, style='->', zorder=3):\n    arrow = FancyArrowPatch(start, end,\n                            arrowstyle=style,\n                            color=color,\n                            linewidth=lw,\n                            mutation_scale=10,\n                            zorder=zorder,\n                            connectionstyle='arc3,rad=0')\n    ax.add_patch(arrow)\n    return arrow\n\n\nfig = plt.figure(figsize=(16, 5))\ngs = fig.add_gridspec(1, 3, width_ratios=[1, 1.05, 1], wspace=0.35)\n\n# ================================================================\n# Panel (a): The Puzzle\n# ================================================================\nax_a = fig.add_subplot(gs[0])\nax_a.set_xlim(0, 1)\nax_a.set_ylim(0, 1)\nax_a.axis('off')\n\nax_a.text(0.0, 1.02, '(a)', fontsize=13, fontweight='bold', transform=ax_a.transAxes,\n          va='bottom', ha='left', color=C_DARK)\nax_a.text(0.08, 1.02, 'The Puzzle', fontsize=11, fontstyle='italic', transform=ax_a.transAxes,\n          va='bottom', ha='left', color=C_GRAY)\n\n# Camera icon (simplified as a rounded box with lens circle)\ncam_x, cam_y = 0.5, 0.92\ncam_w, cam_h = 0.18, 0.07\ncam = FancyBboxPatch((cam_x - cam_w/2, cam_y - cam_h/2), cam_w, cam_h,\n                      boxstyle=\"round,pad=0.008\", facecolor='#EAECEE',\n                      edgecolor=C_LGRAY, linewidth=0.8, zorder=2)\ncam.set_path_effects(SHADOW_PROPS)\nax_a.add_patch(cam)\nlens = plt.Circle((cam_x, cam_y), 0.018, facecolor='white', edgecolor=C_LGRAY,\n                   linewidth=0.8, zorder=3)\nax_a.add_artist(lens)\nax_a.text(cam_x, cam_y + cam_h/2 + 0.025, 'Robot Observation', fontsize=8,\n          ha='center', va='bottom', color=C_GRAY)\n\n# Three branches\nbranches = [\n    {'name': 'Linear\\nProjection', 'x': 0.15, 'border': '#A8D5BA',\n     'fill': '#A8D5BA', 'cv': 'CV = 0.055', 'pattern': 'uniform'},\n    {'name': 'MLP\\nProjector', 'x': 0.50, 'border': '#F5CBA7',\n     'fill': '#E69F00', 'cv': 'CV = 0.215', 'pattern': 'gradient'},\n    {'name': 'Pixel-\\nShuffle', 'x': 0.85, 'border': '#F1948A',\n     'fill': '#D55E00', 'cv': 'CV = 0.314', 'pattern': 'heterogeneous'},\n]\n\nconnector_y = 0.66\nsq_y = 0.42\nsq_size = 0.032\nsq_gap = 0.004\nn_sq = 8\n\nfor b in branches:\n    bx = b['x']\n    bw = 0.22\n    bh = 0.10\n\n    # Arrow from camera to connector box\n    draw_arrow(ax_a, (cam_x, cam_y - cam_h/2 - 0.01),\n               (bx, connector_y + bh/2 + 0.01),\n               color=C_LGRAY, lw=0.7, style='->')\n\n    # Connector box\n    box = FancyBboxPatch((bx - bw/2, connector_y - bh/2), bw, bh,\n                          boxstyle=\"round,pad=0.012\",\n                          facecolor='white', edgecolor=b['border'],\n                          linewidth=0.9, zorder=2)\n    box.set_path_effects(SHADOW_PROPS)\n    ax_a.add_patch(box)\n    ax_a.text(bx, connector_y, b['name'], fontsize=8.5, ha='center', va='center',\n              color=C_DARK, fontweight='bold', linespacing=1.1, zorder=3)\n\n    # Arrow from connector box to squares\n    draw_arrow(ax_a, (bx, connector_y - bh/2 - 0.01),\n               (bx, sq_y + sq_size + 0.015),\n               color=b['border'], lw=0.6, style='->')\n\n    # Token importance squares\n    total_w = n_sq * sq_size + (n_sq - 1) * sq_gap\n    start_x = bx - total_w / 2\n\n    for i in range(n_sq):\n        sx = start_x + i * (sq_size + sq_gap)\n        if b['pattern'] == 'uniform':\n            alpha = 0.55 + 0.05 * np.sin(i * 0.5)\n            color = b['fill']\n        elif b['pattern'] == 'gradient':\n            alpha = 1.0 - i * 0.1\n            color = b['fill']\n        else:  # heterogeneous\n            alpha = [1.0, 0.15, 0.85, 0.12, 0.95, 0.18, 0.78, 0.10][i]\n            color = b['fill']\n\n        rect = FancyBboxPatch((sx, sq_y), sq_size, sq_size,\n                               boxstyle=\"round,pad=0.003\",\n                               facecolor=color, edgecolor='none',\n                               alpha=alpha, linewidth=0, zorder=2)\n        ax_a.add_patch(rect)\n\n    # CV label\n    ax_a.text(bx, sq_y - 0.04, b['cv'], fontsize=8, ha='center', va='top',\n              color=C_GRAY, family='DejaVu Sans Mono')\n\n# Bottom bracket\nbracket_y = 0.18\nax_a.annotate('', xy=(0.04, bracket_y), xytext=(0.04, bracket_y + 0.06),\n              arrowprops=dict(arrowstyle='-', color=C_GRAY, lw=0.5))\nax_a.annotate('', xy=(0.96, bracket_y), xytext=(0.96, bracket_y + 0.06),\n              arrowprops=dict(arrowstyle='-', sub_fontsize=8, text_color=C_DARK,\n                    zorder=2, pad=0.015, shadow=True):\n    x, y = xy\n    box = FancyBboxPatch((x, y), width, height,\n                         boxstyle=f\"round,pad={pad}\",\n                         facecolor=facecolor, edgecolor=edgecolor,\n                         linewidth=linewidth, zorder=zorder,\n                         mutation_aspect=1.0)\n    if shadow:\n        box.set_path_effects(SHADOW_PROPS)\n    ax.add_patch(box)\n    if text:\n        ax.text(x + width / 2, y + height * 0.6, text,\n                fontsize=fontsize, fontweight='bold', ha='center', va='center',\n                color=text_color, zorder=zorder + 1)\n    if subtitle:\n        ax.text(x + width / 2, y + height * 0.25, subtitle,\n return arrow\n\n\nfig = plt.figure(figsize=(16, 5))\ngs = fig.add_gridspec(1, 3, width_ratios=[1, 1.05, 1], wspace=0.35)\n\n# ================================================================\n# Panel Diagnostic', subtitle='One forward pass',\n                fontsize=12, text_color=C_DARK)\n\n# Three outcome boxes\noutcomes = [\n    {'x': 0.17, 'label': 'CV < 0.1', 'name': 'Uniform', 'method': 'L2 norm pruning',\n     'bg': C_GREEN_BG, 'bd': C_GREEN_BD, 'tc': C_GREEN_TXT},\n    {'x': 0.50, 'label': '0.15–0.22', 'name': 'Graded', 'method': 'Attention scoring',\n     'bg': C_YELLOW_BG, 'bd': C_YELLOW_BD, 'tc': C_YELLOW_TXT},\n    {'x': 0.83, 'label': 'CV > 0.3', 'name': 'Catastrophic', 'method': 'VTR re-encoding',\n     'bg': C_RED_BG, 'bd': C_RED_BD, 'tc': C_RED_TXT},\n]\n\nout_y = 0.28\nout_w = 0.26\nout_h = 0.16\n\nfor o in outcomes:\n    # Arrow from diagnostic box to outcome\n    arrow_start = (diag_x + (o['x'] - diag_x) * 0.3, diag_y - diag_h/2 - 0.01)\n    arrow_end = (o['x'], out_y + out_h + 0.01)\n\n    draw_arrow(ax_c, arrow_start, arrow_end, color='#AAAAAA', lw=0.8, style='->')\n\n    # CV range label on arrow\n    mid_ax = (arrow_start[0] + arrow_end[0]) / 2\n    mid_ay = (arrow_start[1] + arrow_end[1]) / 2\n    # offset label to side\n    offset_x = -0.02 if o['x'] < 0.5 else (0.02 if o['x'] > 0.5 else 0)\n    ax_c.text(mid_ax + offset_x, mid_ay + 0.02, o['label'],\n              fontsize=7.5, ha='center', va='center', color=C_GRAY,\n              bbox=dict(facecolor='white', edgecolor='none', pad=1.5, alpha=0.85),\n              zorder=4)\n\n    # Outcome box\n    add_rounded_box(ax_c, (o['x'] - out_w/2, out_y), out_w, out_h,\n                    facecolor=o['bg'], edgecolor=o['bd'], linewidth=0.7,\n                    text=o['name'], subtitle=o['method'],\n                    fontsize=11, sub_fontsize=8, text_color=o['tc'])\n\n# ================================================================\n# Save\n# ================================================================\nfig.savefig(f'{OUTDIR}/fig_hero_overview.png')\nfig.savefig(f'{OUTDIR}/fig_hero_overview.pdf')\nplt.close()\nprint('Saved fig_hero_overview.png and fig_hero_overview.pdf')\n","structuredPatch":[],"originalFile":null,"userModified":false}}

matplotlib\nmatplotlib.use('Agg')\nimport matplotlib.pyplot as plt\nimport matplotlib.patches as mpatches\nfrom matplotlib.patches import FancyBboxPatch, FancyArrowPatch\nimport matplotlib.patheffects as pe\nimport numpy as np\n\nplt.rcParams.update({\n    'font.family': 'DejaVu Sans',\n    'font.size': 10,\n    'figure.dpi': 300,\n    'savefig.dpi': 300,\n    'savefig.bbox': 'tight',\n    'savefig.pad_inches': 0.08,\n    'pdf.fonttype': 42,\n    'ps.fonttype': 42,\n})\n\nOUTDIR = '/home/ubuntu/.agent-ml-research-be-idea-gen-0515-2/projects/importance_margin_vla_compress/docs/paper/figures/paper'\n\n# Colors\nC_GREEN_BG = '#D5F5E3'\nC_GREEN_BD = '#27AE60'\nC_GREEN_TXT = '#1E8449'\nC_YELLOW_BG = '#FCF3CF'\nC_YELLOW_BD = '#F39C12'\nC_YELLOW_TXT = '#B7950B'\nC_ORANGE_BG = '#FDEBD0'\nC_ORANGE_BD = '#E67E22'\nC_ORANGE_TXT = '#CA6F1E'\nC_RED_BG = '#FADBD8'\nC_RED_BD = '#E74C3C'\nC_RED_TXT = '#C0392B'\nC_BLUE_BG = '#D6EAF8'\nC_BLUE_BD = '#3498DB'\nC_DARK = '#2C3E50'\nC_GRAY = '#7F8C8D'\nC_LGRAY = '#BDC3C7'\n\nSHADOW = [pe.withSimplePatchShadow(offset=(1.5, -1.5), shadow_rgbFace='#cccccc', alpha=0.12)]\n\n\ndef rounded_box(ax, xy, w, h, fc, ec, lw=0.6, text=None, sub=None,\n                fs=11, sfs=8, tc=C_DARK, zorder=2, shadow=True):\n    x, y = xy\n    box = FancyBboxPatch((x, y), w, h, boxstyle=\"round,pad=0.015\",\n                         facecolor=fc, edgecolor=ec, linewidth=lw, zorder=zorder)\n    if shadow:\n        box.set_path_effects(SHADOW)\n    ax.add_patch(box)\n    if text:\n        ty = y + h * 0.62 if sub else y + h * 0.5\n        ax.text(x + w/2, ty, text, fontsize=fs, fontweight='bold',\n                ha='center', va='center', color=tc, zorder=zorder+1)\n    if sub:\n        ax.text(x + w/2, y + h * 0.27, sub, fontsize=sfs, ha='center',\n                va='center', color=C_GRAY, fontstyle='italic', zorder=zorder+1)\n    return box\n\n\ndef arrow(ax, s, e, color='#AAAAAA', lw=0.8, style='->', ms=10, zorder=3):\n    a = FancyArrowPatch(s, e, arrowstyle=style, color=color, linewidth=lw,\n                        mutation_scale=ms, zorder=zorder)\n    ax.add_patch(a)\n    return a\n\n\nfig = plt.figure(figsize=(16, 5.2))\ngs = fig.add_gridspec(1, 3, width_ratios=[1, 1.05, 1], wspace=0.32)\n\n# ================================================================\n# Panel (a): The Puzzle\n# ================================================================\nax_a = fig.add_subplot(gs[0])\nax_a.set_xlim(-0.02, 1.02)\nax_a.set_ylim(-0.05, 1.05)\nax_a.axis('off')\n\nax_a.text(0.0, 1.05, '(a)', fontsize=13, fontweight='bold',\n          transform=ax_a.transAxes, va='top', ha='left', color=C_DARK)\nax_a.text(0.10, 1.05, 'The Puzzle', fontsize=11, fontstyle='italic',\n          transform=ax_a.transAxes, va='top', ha='left', color=C_GRAY)\n\n# Camera/observation icon\ncam_cx, cam_cy = 0.50, 0.93\ncam_w, cam_h = 0.22, 0.065\ncam = FancyBboxPatch((cam_cx - cam_w/2, cam_cy - cam_h/2), cam_w, cam_h,\n                      boxstyle=\"round,pad=0.008\", facecolor='#F2F3F4',\n                      edgecolor=C_LGRAY, linewidth=0.8, zorder=2)\ncam.set_path_effects(SHADOW)\nax_a.add_patch(cam)\nlens = plt.Circle((cam_cx, cam_cy), 0.02, facecolor='white',\n                   edgecolor=C_LGRAY, linewidth=0.8, zorder=3)\nax_a.add_artist(lens)\ninner = plt.Circle((cam_cx, cam_cy), 0.008, facecolor=C_LGRAY,\n                    edgecolor='none', zorder=4)\nax_a.add_artist(inner)\nax_a.text(cam_cx, cam_cy + cam_h/2 + 0.025, 'Robot Observation',\n          fontsize=8.5, ha='center', va='bottom', color=C_GRAY)\n\n# Three connector branches\nbranches = [\n    {'name': 'Linear\\nProjection', 'x': 0.15, 'border': '#7DCEA0', 'fill': '#82C99B',\n     'cv': 'CV = 0.055', 'pattern': 'uniform'},\n    {'name': 'MLP\\nProjector', 'x': 0.50, 'border': '#F0B27A', 'fill': '#E69F00',\n     'cv': 'CV = 0.215', 'pattern': 'gradient'},\n    {'name': 'Pixel-\\nShuffle', 'x': 0.85, 'border': '#EC7063', 'fill': '#D55E00',\n     'cv': 'CV = 0.314', 'pattern': 'heterogeneous'},\n]\n\nconn_y = 0.68\nconn_w, conn_h = 0.20, 0.10\nsq_y = 0.38\nsq_size = 0.042\nsq_gap = 0.006\nn_sq = 8\n\nfor b in branches:\n    bx = b['x']\n\n    # Arrow from camera to connector\n    arrow(ax_a, (cam_cx, cam_cy - cam_h/2 - 0.005),\n          (bx, conn_y + conn_h/2 + 0.008),\n          color=C_LGRAY, lw=0.7, style='->', ms=8)\n\n    # Connector box\n    box = FancyBboxPatch((bx - conn_w/2, conn_y - conn_h/2), conn_w, conn_h,\n                          boxstyle=\"round,pad=0.012\", facecolor='white',\n                          edgecolor=b['border'], linewidth=1.0, zorder=2)\n    box.set_path_effects(SHADOW)\n    ax_a.add_patch(box)\n    ax_a.text(bx, conn_y + 0.002, b['name'], fontsize=8.5, ha='center',\n              va='center', color=C_DARK, fontweight='bold', linespacing=1.1, zorder=3)\n\n    # Arrow from connector to squares\n    arrow(ax_a, (bx, conn_y - conn_h/2 - 0.005),\n          (bx, sq_y + sq_size + 0.012),\n          color=b['border'], lw=0.6, style='->', ms=8)\n\n    # Token importance squares\n    total_w = n_sq * sq_size + (n_sq - 1) * sq_gap\n    sx_start = bx - total_w / 2\n\n    for i in range(n_sq):\n        sx = sx_start + i * (sq_size + sq_gap)\n        if b['pattern'] == 'uniform':\n            alpha_val = 0.50 + 0.04 * np.sin(i * 0.8)\n        elif b['pattern'] == 'gradient':\n            alpha_val = 0.95 - i * 0.09\n        else:\n            alpha_val = [1.0, 0.12, 0.90, 0.10, 0.95, 0.15, 0.80, 0.08][i]\n\n        rect = FancyBboxPatch((sx, sq_y), sq_size, sq_size,\n                               boxstyle=\"round,pad=0.003\",\n                               facecolor=b['fill'], edgecolor='#dddddd',\n                               alpha=alpha_val, linewidth=0.3, zorder=2)\n        ax_a.add_patch(rect)\n\n    # CV value\n    ax_a.text(bx, sq_y - 0.035, b['cv'], fontsize=8, ha='center', va='top',\n              color=C_GRAY, family='DejaVu Sans Mono')\n\n# Bottom bracket\nbk_y = 0.14\nbk_h = 0.05\nax_a.plot([0.04, 0.04], [bk_y + bk_h, bk_y], color=C_LGRAY, lw=0.6)\nax_a.plot([0.04, 0.50], [bk_y, bk_y - 0.02], color=C_LGRAY, lw=0.6)\nax_a.plot([0.96, 0.96], [bk_y + bk_h, bk_y], color=C_LGRAY, lw=0.6)\nax_a.plot([0.96, 0.50], [bk_y, bk_y - 0.02], color=C_LGRAY, lw=0.6)\n\nax_a.text(0.50, bk_y - 0.07, 'Same input, different compressibility',\n          fontsize=9, ha='center', va='top', color=C_GRAY, fontstyle='italic')\n\n# ================================================================\n# Panel (b): Two-Axis Framework\n# ================================================================\nax_b = fig.add_subplot(gs[1])\nax_b.set_xlim(-0.05, 1.10)\nax_b.set_ylim(-0.10, 1.05)\nax_b.axis('off')\n\nax_b.text(0.0, 1.05, '(b)', fontsize=13, fontweight='bold',\n          transform=ax_b.transAxes, va='top', ha='left', color=C_DARK)\nax_b.text(0.10, 1.05, 'Two-Axis Framework', fontsize=11, fontstyle='italic',\n          transform=ax_b.transAxes, va='top', ha='left', color=C_GRAY)\n\n# Grid parameters\ngx0, gy0 = 0.15, 0.12\ngw, gh = 0.34, 0.34\nggap = 0.04\n\n# cells: (col, row) where row=0=Simple(bottom), row=1=Complex(top)\ncells = [\n    # col, row, bg, border, main, main_color, subtitle, hatching\n    (0, 0, C_GREEN_BG, C_GREEN_BD, '≥98% SR', C_GREEN_TXT, 'Tolerant', False),\n    (0, 1, C_YELLOW_BG, C_YELLOW_BD, '~89% SR', C_YELLOW_TXT, 'Moderate loss', False),\n    (1, 0, C_ORANGE_BG, C_ORANGE_BD, 'Cliff at k≤0.7', C_ORANGE_TXT, 'Threshold', False),\n    (1, 1, C_RED_BG, C_RED_BD, '0–48% SR',            ha='center', va='center', color=mc, zorder=3)\n    ax_b.text(cx + gw/2, cy + gh * 0.30, sub, fontsize=8, ha='center',\n              va='center', color=C_GRAY, fontstyle='italic', zorder=3)\n\n# Horizontal axis label\nmid_x = gx0 + gw + ggap/2\nax_b.text(mid_x, gy0 - 0.07, 'Token Coupling', fontsize=11, ha='center',\n          va='top', fontweight='bold', color=C_DARK)\nax_b.text(gx0 + gw/2, gy0 - 0.03, 'Independent', fontsize=8.5, ha='center',\n          va='top', color=C_GRAY)\nax_b.text(gx0 + gw + ggap + gw/2, gy0 - 0.03, 'Entangling', 'VTR re-encoding',\n     'bg': C_RED_BG, 'bd': C_RED_BD, 'tc': C_RED_TXT},\n]\n\nout_w, out_h = 0.27, 0.18\nout_y = 0.22\n\nfor o in outcomes:\n    # Arrow\n    a_start_x = 0.50 + (o['x'] - 0.50) * range label on the arrow\n    lx = (a_start_x + o['x']) / 2\n    ly = (a_start_y + a_end_y) / 2 + 0.015\n    dx = -0.04 if o['x'] < 0.4 else (0.04 if o['x'] > 0.6 else 0.0)\n    ax_c.text(lx + dx, ly, o['label'], fontsize=7.5, ha='center', va='center',\n              color=C_GRAY,\n              bbox=dict(facecolor='white', edgecolor='none', pad=1.5, alpha=0.9),\n              zorder=4)\n\n    # Outcome box\n    rounded_box(ax_c, (o['x'] - out_w/2, out_y), out_w, out_h,\n                fc=o['bg'], ec=o['bd'], lw=0.7,\n                text=o['name'], sub=o['method'],\n                fs=11,
