matplotlib\nmatplotlib.use('Agg')\nimport matplotlib.pyplot as plt\nimport matplotlib.patches as mpatches\nfrom matplotlib.patches import FancyBboxPatch, FancyArrowPatch\nimport matplotlib.patheffects as pe\nimport numpy as np\n\nplt.rcParams.update({\n    'font.family': 'DejaVu Sans',\n    'font.size': 10,\n    'figure.dpi': 300,\n    'savefig.dpi': 300,\n    'savefig.bbox': 'tight',\n    'savefig.pad_inches': 0.08,\n    'pdf.fonttype': 42,\n    'ps.fonttype': 42,\n})\n\nOUTDIR = '/home/ubuntu/.agent-ml-research-be-idea-gen-0515-2/projects/importance_margin_vla_compress/docs/paper/figures/paper'\n\n# Colors\nC_GREEN_BG = '#D5F5E3'\nC_GREEN_BD = '#27AE60'\nC_GREEN_TXT = '#1E8449'\nC_YELLOW_BG = '#FCF3CF'\nC_YELLOW_BD = '#F39C12'\nC_YELLOW_TXT = '#B7950B'\nC_ORANGE_BG = '#FDEBD0'\nC_ORANGE_BD = '#E67E22'\nC_ORANGE_TXT = '#CA6F1E'\nC_RED_BG = '#FADBD8'\nC_RED_BD = '#E74C3C'\nC_RED_TXT = '#C0392B'\nC_BLUE_BG = '#D6EAF8'\nC_BLUE_BD = '#3498DB'\nC_DARK = '#2C3E50'\nC_GRAY = '#7F8C8D'\nC_LGRAY = '#BDC3C7'\n\nSHADOW = [pe.withSimplePatchShadow(offset=(1.5, -1.5), shadow_rgbFace='#cccccc', alpha=0.12)]\n\n\ndef rounded_box(ax, xy, w, h, fc, ec, lw=0.6, text=None, sub=None,\n                fs=11, sfs=8, tc=C_DARK, zorder=2, shadow=True):\n    x, y = xy\n    box = FancyBboxPatch((x, y), w, h, boxstyle=\"round,pad=0.015\",\n                         facecolor=fc, edgecolor=ec, linewidth=lw, zorder=zorder)\n    if shadow:\n        box.set_path_effects(SHADOW)\n    ax.add_patch(box)\n    if text:\n        ty = y + h * 0.62 if sub else y + h * 0.5\n        ax.text(x + w/2, ty, text, fontsize=fs, fontweight='bold',\n                ha='center', va='center', color=tc, zorder=zorder+1)\n    if sub:\n        ax.text(x + w/2, y + h * 0.27, sub, fontsize=sfs, ha='center',\n                va='center', color=C_GRAY, fontstyle='italic', zorder=zorder+1)\n    return box\n\n\ndef arrow(ax, s, e, color='#AAAAAA', lw=0.8, style='->', ms=10, zorder=3):\n    a = FancyArrowPatch(s, e, arrowstyle=style, color=color, linewidth=lw,\n                        mutation_scale=ms, zorder=zorder)\n    ax.add_patch(a)\n    return a\n\n\nfig = plt.figure(figsize=(16, 5.2))\ngs = fig.add_gridspec(1, 3, width_ratios=[1, 1.05, 1], wspace=0.32)\n\n# ================================================================\n# Panel (a): The Puzzle\n# ================================================================\nax_a = fig.add_subplot(gs[0])\nax_a.set_xlim(-0.02, 1.02)\nax_a.set_ylim(-0.05, 1.05)\nax_a.axis('off')\n\nax_a.text(0.0, 1.05, '(a)', fontsize=13, fontweight='bold',\n          transform=ax_a.transAxes, va='top', ha='left', color=C_DARK)\nax_a.text(0.10, 1.05, 'The Puzzle', fontsize=11, fontstyle='italic',\n          transform=ax_a.transAxes, va='top', ha='left', color=C_GRAY)\n\n# Camera/observation icon\ncam_cx, cam_cy = 0.50, 0.93\ncam_w, cam_h = 0.22, 0.065\ncam = FancyBboxPatch((cam_cx - cam_w/2, cam_cy - cam_h/2), cam_w, cam_h,\n                      boxstyle=\"round,pad=0.008\", facecolor='#F2F3F4',\n                      edgecolor=C_LGRAY, linewidth=0.8, zorder=2)\ncam.set_path_effects(SHADOW)\nax_a.add_patch(cam)\nlens = plt.Circle((cam_cx, cam_cy), 0.02, facecolor='white',\n                   edgecolor=C_LGRAY, linewidth=0.8, zorder=3)\nax_a.add_artist(lens)\ninner = plt.Circle((cam_cx, cam_cy), 0.008, facecolor=C_LGRAY,\n                    edgecolor='none', zorder=4)\nax_a.add_artist(inner)\nax_a.text(cam_cx, cam_cy + cam_h/2 + 0.025, 'Robot Observation',\n          fontsize=8.5, ha='center', va='bottom', color=C_GRAY)\n\n# Three connector branches\nbranches = [\n    {'name': 'Linear\\nProjection', 'x': 0.15, 'border': '#7DCEA0', 'fill': '#82C99B',\n     'cv': 'CV = 0.055', 'pattern': 'uniform'},\n    {'name': 'MLP\\nProjector', 'x': 0.50, 'border': '#F0B27A', 'fill': '#E69F00',\n     'cv': 'CV = 0.215', 'pattern': 'gradient'},\n    {'name': 'Pixel-\\nShuffle', 'x': 0.85, 'border': '#EC7063', 'fill': '#D55E00',\n     'cv': 'CV = 0.314', 'pattern': 'heterogeneous'},\n]\n\nconn_y = 0.68\nconn_w, conn_h = 0.20, 0.10\nsq_y = 0.38\nsq_size = 0.042\nsq_gap = 0.006\nn_sq = 8\n\nfor b in branches:\n    bx = b['x']\n\n    # Arrow from camera to connector\n    arrow(ax_a, (cam_cx, cam_cy - cam_h/2 - 0.005),\n          (bx, conn_y + conn_h/2 + 0.008),\n          color=C_LGRAY, lw=0.7, style='->', ms=8)\n\n    # Connector box\n    box = FancyBboxPatch((bx - conn_w/2, conn_y - conn_h/2), conn_w, conn_h,\n                          boxstyle=\"round,pad=0.012\", facecolor='white',\n                          edgecolor=b['border'], linewidth=1.0, zorder=2)\n    box.set_path_effects(SHADOW)\n    ax_a.add_patch(box)\n    ax_a.text(bx, conn_y + 0.002, b['name'], fontsize=8.5, ha='center',\n              va='center', color=C_DARK, fontweight='bold', linespacing=1.1, zorder=3)\n\n    # Arrow from connector to squares\n    arrow(ax_a, (bx, conn_y - conn_h/2 - 0.005),\n          (bx, sq_y + sq_size + 0.012),\n          color=b['border'], lw=0.6, style='->', ms=8)\n\n    # Token importance squares\n    total_w = n_sq * sq_size + (n_sq - 1) * sq_gap\n    sx_start = bx - total_w / 2\n\n    for i in range(n_sq):\n        sx = sx_start + i * (sq_size + sq_gap)\n        if b['pattern'] == 'uniform':\n            alpha_val = 0.50 + 0.04 * np.sin(i * 0.8)\n        elif b['pattern'] == 'gradient':\n            alpha_val = 0.95 - i * 0.09\n        else:\n            alpha_val = [1.0, 0.12, 0.90, 0.10, 0.95, 0.15, 0.80, 0.08][i]\n\n        rect = FancyBboxPatch((sx, sq_y), sq_size, sq_size,\n                               boxstyle=\"round,pad=0.003\",\n                               facecolor=b['fill'], edgecolor='#dddddd',\n                               alpha=alpha_val, linewidth=0.3, zorder=2)\n        ax_a.add_patch(rect)\n\n    # CV value\n    ax_a.text(bx, sq_y - 0.035, b['cv'], fontsize=8, ha='center', va='top',\n              color=C_GRAY, family='DejaVu Sans Mono')\n\n# Bottom bracket\nbk_y = 0.14\nbk_h = 0.05\nax_a.plot([0.04, 0.04], [bk_y + bk_h, bk_y], color=C_LGRAY, lw=0.6)\nax_a.plot([0.04, 0.50], [bk_y, bk_y - 0.02], color=C_LGRAY, lw=0.6)\nax_a.plot([0.96, 0.96], [bk_y + bk_h, bk_y], color=C_LGRAY, lw=0.6)\nax_a.plot([0.96, 0.50], [bk_y, bk_y - 0.02], color=C_LGRAY, lw=0.6)\n\nax_a.text(0.50, bk_y - 0.07, 'Same input, different compressibility',\n          fontsize=9, ha='center', va='top', color=C_GRAY, fontstyle='italic')\n\n# ================================================================\n# Panel (b): Two-Axis Framework\n# ================================================================\nax_b = fig.add_subplot(gs[1])\nax_b.set_xlim(-0.05, 1.10)\nax_b.set_ylim(-0.10, 1.05)\nax_b.axis('off')\n\nax_b.text(0.0, 1.05, '(b)', fontsize=13, fontweight='bold',\n          transform=ax_b.transAxes, va='top', ha='left', color=C_DARK)\nax_b.text(0.10, 1.05, 'Two-Axis Framework', fontsize=11, fontstyle='italic',\n          transform=ax_b.transAxes, va='top', ha='left', color=C_GRAY)\n\n# Grid parameters\ngx0, gy0 = 0.15, 0.12\ngw, gh = 0.34, 0.34\nggap = 0.04\n\n# cells: (col, row) where row=0=Simple(bottom), row=1=Complex(top)\ncells = [\n    # col, row, bg, border, main, main_color, subtitle, hatching\n    (0, 0, C_GREEN_BG, C_GREEN_BD, '≥98% SR', C_GREEN_TXT, 'Tolerant', False),\n    (0, 1, C_YELLOW_BG, C_YELLOW_BD, '~89% SR', C_YELLOW_TXT, 'Moderate loss', False),\n    (1, 0, C_ORANGE_BG, C_ORANGE_BD, 'Cliff at k≤0.7', C_ORANGE_TXT, 'Threshold', False),\n    (1, 1, C_RED_BG, C_RED_BD, '0–48% SR', C_RED_TXT, 'Catastrophic', True),\n]\n\nfor col, row, bg, bd, main, mc, sub, hatch in cells:\n    cx = gx0 + col * (gw + ggap)\n    cy = gy0 + row * (gh + ggap)  # row=0 bottom, row=1 top\n\n    box = FancyBboxPatch((cx, cy), gw, gh, boxstyle=\"round,pad=0.012\",\n                          facecolor=bg, edgecolor=bd, linewidth=0.6, zorder=2)\n    box.set_path_effects(SHADOW)\n    ax_b.add_patch(box)\n\n    if hatch:\n        hb = FancyBboxPatch((cx, cy), gw, gh, boxstyle=\"round,pad=0.012\",\n                             facecolor='none', edgecolor='none',\n                             hatch='///', alpha=0.10, zorder=2.5)\n        ax_b.add_patch(hb)\n\n    fs = 11 if len(main) <= 10 else 9.5\n    ax_b.text(cx + gw/2, cy + gh * 0.58, main, fontsize=fs, fontweight='bold',\n              ha='center', va='center', color=mc, zorder=3)\n    ax_b.text(cx + gw/2, cy + gh * 0.30, sub, fontsize=8, ha='center',\n              va='center', color=C_GRAY, fontstyle='italic', zorder=3)\n\n# Horizontal axis label\nmid_x = gx0 + gw + ggap/2\nax_b.text(mid_x, gy0 - 0.07, 'Token Coupling', fontsize=11, ha='center',\n          va='top', fontweight='bold', color=C_DARK)\nax_b.text(gx0 + gw/2, gy0 - 0.03, 'Independent', fontsize=8.5, ha='center',\n          va='top', color=C_GRAY)\nax_b.text(gx0 + gw + ggap + gw/2, gy0 - 0.03, 'Entangling', fontsize=8.5,\n          ha='center', va='top', color=C_GRAY)\n\n# Vertical axis label\nax_b.text(gx0 - 0.10, gy0 + gh + ggap/2, 'Task\\nComplexity', fontsize=11,\n          ha='center', va='center', fontweight='bold', color=C_DARK, rotation=90,\n          linespacing=1.2)\nax_b.text(gx0 - 0.04, gy0 + gh/2, 'Simple', fontsize=8.5, ha='center',\n          va='center', color=C_GRAY, rotation=90)\nax_b.text(gx0 - 0.04, gy0 + gh + ggap + gh/2, 'Complex', fontsize=8.5,\n          ha='center', va='center', color=C_GRAY, rotation=90)\n\n# Diagonal risk arrow (bottom-left corner → top-right corner)\nar_x0 = gx0 + gw + ggap + gw + 0.05\nar_y0 = gy0\nar_y1 = gy0 + 2 * gh + ggap\narrow(ax_b, (ar_x0, ar_y0), (ar_x0, ar_y1),\n      color=C_RED_BD, lw=1.2, style='->', ms=12)\nax_b.text(ar_x0 + 0.04, (ar_y0 + ar_y1)/2, 'Increasing\\npruning risk',\n          fontsize=7.5, ha='left', va='center', color=C_GRAY,\n          fontstyle='italic', rotation=90, linespacing=1.3)\n\n# Small colored dots on the arrow for gradient effect\nfor frac, c in [(0.0, C_GREEN_BD), (0.33, C_YELLOW_BD), (0.66, C_ORANGE_BD), (1.0, C_RED_BD)]:\n    y_dot = ar_y0 + frac * (ar_y1 - ar_y0)\n    ax_b.plot(ar_x0, y_dot, 'o', color=c, markersize=3, zorder=4)\n\n# ================================================================\n# Panel (c): Diagnose-then-Compress\n# ================================================================\nax_c = fig.add_subplot(gs[2])\nax_c.set_xlim(-0.02, 1.02)\nax_c.set_ylim(-0.02, 1.05)\nax_c.axis('off')\n\nax_c.text(0.0, 1.05, '(c)', fontsize=13, fontweight='bold',\n          transform=ax_c.transAxes, va='top', ha='left', color=C_DARK)\nax_c.text(0.10, 1.05, 'Diagnose-then-Compress', fontsize=11, fontstyle='italic',\n          transform=ax_c.transAxes, va='top', ha='left', color=C_GRAY)\n\n# Top: CV Diagnostic box\ndiag_w, diag_h = 0.48, 0.14\ndiag_x = 0.50 - diag_w/2\ndiag_y = 0.78\nrounded_box(ax_c, (diag_x, diag_y), diag_w, diag_h,\n            fc=C_BLUE_BG, ec=C_BLUE_BD, lw=0.9,\n            text='CV Diagnostic', sub='One forward pass',\n            fs=12, sfs=8.5, tc=C_DARK)\n\n# Three outcome branches\noutcomes = [\n    {'x': 0.16, 'label': 'CV < 0.1', 'name': 'Uniform', 'method': 'L2 norm pruning',\n     'bg': C_GREEN_BG, 'bd': C_GREEN_BD, 'tc': C_GREEN_TXT},\n    {'x': 0.50, 'label': '0.15–0.22', 'name': 'Graded', 'method': 'Attention scoring',\n     'bg': C_YELLOW_BG, 'bd': C_YELLOW_BD, 'tc': C_YELLOW_TXT},\n    {'x': 0.84, 'label': 'CV > 0.3', 'name': 'Catastrophic', 'method': 'VTR re-encoding',\n     'bg': C_RED_BG, 'bd': C_RED_BD, 'tc': C_RED_TXT},\n]\n\nout_w, out_h = 0.27, 0.18\nout_y = 0.22\n\nfor o in outcomes:\n    # Arrow\n    a_start_x = 0.50 + (o['x'] - 0.50) * 0.4\n    a_start_y = diag_y - 0.005\n    a_end_y = out_y + out_h + 0.008\n\n    arrow(ax_c, (a_start_x, a_start_y), (o['x'], a_end_y),\n          color='#B0B0B0', lw=0.8, style='->', ms=9)\n\n    # CV range label on the arrow\n    lx = (a_start_x + o['x']) / 2\n    ly = (a_start_y + a_end_y) / 2 + 0.015\n    dx = -0.04 if o['x'] < 0.4 else (0.04 if o['x'] > 0.6 else 0.0)\n    ax_c.text(lx + dx, ly, o['label'], fontsize=7.5, ha='center', va='center',\n              color=C_GRAY,\n              bbox=dict(facecolor='white', edgecolor='none', pad=1.5, alpha=0.9),\n              zorder=4)\n\n    # Outcome box\n    rounded_box(ax_c, (o['x'] - out_w/2, out_y), out_w, out_h,\n                fc=o['bg'], ec=o['bd'], lw=0.7,\n                text=o['name'], sub=o['method'],\n                fs=11, sfs=8, tc=o['tc'])\n\n# ================================================================\n# Save\n# ================================================================\nfig.savefig(f'{OUTDIR}/fig_hero_overview.png')\nfig.savefig(f'{OUTDIR}/fig_hero_overview.pdf')\nplt.close()\nprint('Saved
